### Decisions taken in B3 (D-77)

None of these opens a question. Each row is the most reasonable default within the BUILD_SPEC author's remit, and the items below apply it. Rows marked "interface" name another author's phase; the orchestrator relays them.

| Id | Decision | Rationale |
|---|---|---|
| BS1-D-01 | FND builds `backend/erev_engine/canonical.py` (dev-guide §5.17 complete) and `backend/erev_engine/currencies.py` (`CurrencySpec`, `ISO_4217`). EKC consumes both unchanged. The private exact formatter inside `canonical.py` equals the DG-KRN-MONEY `format_exact` contract (ROUND_HALF_UP at 18 places, trailing zeros trimmed, no exponent); EKC may replace it by a call to `erev_engine.money.format_exact` in a patch that changes no output. Interface: EKC (BS-2) | FND consumes both before EKC runs: `KeyRing.encrypt` uses the canonical bytes of its context as associated data (DG-KRN-KEY-04), and the `currency` seed comes from `ISO_4217` (DG-MIG-07; PHASES §5.1 FND table T-REF-08) |
| BS1-D-02 | Keys and secrets (dev-guide §5.19) are built in FND-4, not PLF | PHASES §6.1 places REQ-SEC-003 in FND, and its statement needs `EnvSecretStore`, per-tenant HKDF and envelope encryption. PHASES governs membership (PHASES header "Precedence") |
| BS1-D-03 | FND-11 builds `UnitOfWork.commit()` steps (1), (3) and (4) of DG-KRN-UOW-02. PLF-3 inserts step (2) `audit.chain.flush` in the commit that creates `audit_event`. FND also defines the `FileStore` protocol and `LocalFileStore` (FND-10); PLF-8 adds `store_file` and `open_file` over T-PLT-29 | A unit of work without audit tables has no events to flush, and XR-19 forbids depending on a later item |
| BS1-D-04 | PLF-14 creates T-INT-03 `outbox_message`. DIN does not create it and uses it as delivered. Interface: DIN (BS-3) | PHASES §1 PLF exit criterion (1) requires the §5.11 outbox, and PHASES §5.3 PLF lists the invitation `outbox_message` among the provisioning rows, while PHASES §5.1 lists the table under DIN. The producer phase must own the table |
| BS1-D-05 | PLF-12 uses `erev_engine.rules.match` (EKC). When `erev_engine.rules.FIELDS` has no entry for `APPROVAL_ROUTING` or `AUTO_APPROVAL`, PLF-12 adds exactly these two entries: `APPROVAL_ROUTING` fields `subject.type`, `entity.code`, `amount.functional`, `flags`; `AUTO_APPROVAL` fields `subject.type`, `preparer.role_codes`, `tenant.setup_completed`, `source.channel`. Existing kinds stay unchanged, and a test proves it. Interface: EKC (BS-2) | 04 T-REF-26 lists fields only for `POB_ASSIGNMENT`, and 04 §14.3 fixes the `AUTO-BOOTSTRAP` conditions. An additive entry changes no existing behaviour (dev-guide §0.4) |

Rev 1.10 (supervisor ruling R-63 (e) as refined by R-115 (c) of 2026-09-30): with `legal_entity` in place the grant routes of PLF-17 and PLF-18 take entity codes through the kernel resolver `erev_api.auth.entity_scope` (04 T-PLT-10 rev 1.148); a code that names no legal entity, or one outside the grantor's own scope, keeps `EREV-REF-002` | XR-12 fail-closed consumers; DG-MIG-03 places a foreign key to a later table in the later table's revision |

## 01 FND Foundation: repository, toolchain, gates and kernel base

### 01.1 Phase preamble

**Entry criteria.** Roles `erev_owner` and `erev_app` and databases `erev`, `erev_test` and `erev_e2e` are provisioned by the supervisor (D-42; DG-ENV-15). `docs/BUILD_SPEC.md` is assembled.

**Exit criteria (PHASES §1 row FND).**

1. The tree and layout rules of dev-guide §1.1 to §1.3 hold: the `backend/` uv project; the `frontend/` toolchain (DG-FE-01, 09, 12, 13, 17, 19, 20; DG-RUN-20 to 24); `scripts/`; `Makefile`; `LICENSE`; `NOTICE`; `README.md`; `.env.example`.
2. Every FND target of PHASES §13 exists with DG-MK-00a to 00h semantics, and `make setup` is idempotent.
3. Kernel: dev-guide §5.1; §5.2 engines, session factories and role guard over global tables; §5.8 with `PROBLEMS` equal to 04 §15.2; §5.9 API money types; §5.10; §5.18 over global tables; §6.4 route helpers; §6.6 logging; API-R-53 `healthz`, `readyz` and `openapi.json`.
4. The revisions for 04 §18 step 0001 and the FND tables of PHASES §5.1 exist and pass DG-MIG-05.
5. `erev registry-seed` covers POLICIES §1 and T-PLT-31; `controls.yaml` equals 03 §4.1; StrEnum mirrors of 04 §3 exist; `CATALOGUE` equals T-PLT-11.
6. Self-tested `design_check`, `vocab_check`, `licence_check`, `secrets_check` and `openapi_check` pass; `make fixtures` has committed legacy fixtures and a manifest (DG-PAR-02).
7. DG-ARC-01 to 06, 09, 10, 12 and 13 exist and pass.
8. The frontend toolchain has `theme-init.js`, the tokens copy and the DS-VER-01 and DS-VER-02 Vitest suites; the router holds no screen until WEB.
9. The guides of BS-D-13 exist with sections for what is built.
10. `make ci` and `make test-pg` are green.

## 03 PLF Platform services

### 03.1 Phase preamble

**Entry criteria.** GATE-EKC is ticked. No other entry criterion (PHASES §1 row PLF).

**Exit criteria (PHASES §1 row PLF).**

1. Kernel: dev-guide §5.2 tenant context; §5.3; §5.4; §5.5 with chain and verification; §5.6 (routing, delegation, stale invalidation, preview snapshot storage); §5.7; §5.11 (outbox, notifications, fake email, webhooks); §5.12 (Procrastinate schema DG-MIG-09, eight queues, sweeper, worker); §5.13; §5.14; §5.15 (versions, resolve; presets are RFD); §5.16 explain service over in-memory traces; §5.19 (delivered in FND, BS1-D-02); §5.20.
2. The PLF tables of PHASES §5.1 exist with RLS templates, IM classes, grants and DB invariants, and DG-TST-20 to DG-TST-25 cover every tenant table.
3. The API-R rows of PHASES §5.2 for PLF exist: argon2id passwords, sessions and CSRF, TOTP and recovery codes, password reset, OIDC against the in-process mock IdP (D-72), OAuth2 API clients, support grants, saved views and access review campaigns.

5. DG-ARC-04, DG-ARC-07 and DG-ARC-08 pass (BS-D-07).
6. The tagged tests of PHASES §7 for PLF exist.
7. `make dev-up`, `make dev-down`, `make status`, `make worker` and `make doctor` exist.

**Gate scope at the exit (PHASES §4 row GATE-PLF).** `make ci`; `make test-pg`; the answer-key loader and coverage unit tests inside `make ci`; `make properties` unfiltered; `erev controls-report --tags-only` inside `make lint`; `make dev-up`, `GET /api/v1/readyz` 200, `make dev-down`; `make doctor`. `make e2e` and `make parity` are not yet run.

**Membership (PHASES §5.1 to §5.3, §6.3, §7, §13).** Tables T-PLT-01 to T-PLT-10, T-PLT-12 to T-PLT-30, T-PLT-32, T-PLT-33, T-PLT-35 to T-PLT-38, T-PLT-40 to T-PLT-42 and T-REF-24 to T-REF-27 (42), plus T-INT-03 (BS1-D-04) and the Procrastinate objects in `public`. Job kinds `AUDIT_CHAIN_VERIFY`, `OUTBOX_RELAY`, `WEBHOOK_DELIVERY`, `EMAIL_DELIVERY`, `RETENTION_SWEEP`. Outbox topics `WEBHOOK`, `EMAIL`.       - `backend/tests/api/test_session.py::test_login_sets_cookie_and_returns_csrf`: `POST /api/v1/session/login` with valid credentials returns 200 with `authenticated` true, a 43-character base64url `csrf_token`, `mfa_required` false and `mfa_enrolment_required` false (BS1-D-30); `Set-Cookie` holds `erev_session=…; HttpOnly; Path=/; SameSite=Lax` without `Secure` for host `127.0.0.1`; `user_session.token_sha256` equals the SHA-256 of the cookie value; `security_event` `LOGIN_SUCCEEDED` is written.
      - `::test_ctl_033_lockout_after_five_failures`: five wrong passwords write `LOGIN_FAILED` five times and `ACCOUNT_LOCKED` once; a sixth attempt with the correct password returns 423 `account-locked`; `app_user.locked_until` equals the FrozenClock time + 15 minutes; after advancing the clock 15 minutes, the correct password returns 200 and `failed_login_count` is 0.
      - `::test_ctl_033_idle_and_absolute_timeout`: 31 minutes after the last request, `POST /session/tenant` returns 401 `session-expired`, `end_reason` is `IDLE_TIMEOUT` and `SESSION_EXPIRED` is written; with a request every 20 minutes, the request at 12 hours 1 minute returns 401 `session-expired` with `end_reason` `ABSOLUTE_TIMEOUT`.
      - `::test_logout_invalidates_session_server_side`: `POST /session/logout` returns 204; replaying the old cookie returns 401 `unauthenticated`; `end_reason` is `LOGOUT` and `LOGOUT` is written.
      - `::test_krn_auth_02_csrf_and_origin`: `POST /session/tenant` with the cookie and no `X-CSRF-Token` returns 403 `forbidden` with `errors[0].rule_id == "API-C-02"`; with the token and `Origin: http://127.0.0.1:9999` it also returns 403.
      - `::test_sar_09_tenant_selection_rotates_token`: `POST /session/tenant {tenant_id}` for an `ACTIVE` membership returns 200 with a new cookie value; the previous session ends `REVOKED`; `TENANT_SELECTED` is written; `tenant_membership.last_opened_at` is set; later responses carry `X-Erev-Tenant-Kind: production`; a tenant without an `ACTIVE` membership returns 404 `not-found`.
      - `::test_sar_13_login_rate_limit`: the 11th login attempt within one minute for one address and email returns 429 `rate-limited` with a `Retry-After` header and writes no `LOGIN_FAILED`.
      - `::test_sar_06_unknown_email`: an unknown email returns 401 `unauthenticated` with detail `The email or password is incorrect.`; `LOGIN_FAILED` has `user_id` null and `email_sha256` set; argon2 verification runs once against the dummy hash.
      - `::test_get_session_without_cookie_returns_capabilities`: `GET /session` without a cookie returns 200 `{"authenticated": false, "capabilities": {"identity_providers": []}}`.
      - `backend/tests/api/test_auth_before_body.py` (rev 1.36; item AUTH-BEFORE-BODY-1; 05 §2.5 rev 1.203; 04 API-C-17 rev 1.284): `::test_auth_before_body_1_a_caller_without_a_session_is_answered_401_whatever_the_body` — every operation of the application that changes something and asks a session question answers a caller without a session 401 for a body that decodes, one that does not, one of another media type and none, and so a cookie that names no session and a bearer token that names no client (403 at an operator route); `::test_auth_before_body_1_an_operation_without_a_guard_still_reads_its_body` and `::test_auth_before_body_1_a_signed_in_callers_body_is_still_judged` — the positive controls; `::test_auth_before_body_1_the_identity_store_is_asked_once_a_request` — a JSON command, a read and `POST /files` each send one statement that looks the session up; `::test_auth_before_body_1_a_kept_answer_does_not_outlive_a_slow_body` and `::test_auth_before_body_1_an_upload_whose_session_ended_stores_nothing` — a session ended while a body took longer than a second of the application clock is refused when the command starts, and nothing is changed or stored; `::test_auth_before_body_1_a_guard_asked_after_the_session_ended_reads_the_stores_refusal` — a handler that ended the session it was called with and forgot the kept answer is refused by the store when it asks again, and one that did not forget is served the answer of the ended session, which is what the forgetting is for.
      - `backend/tests/unit/test_session_questions.py` (rev 1.36; dev-guide DG-KRN-AUTH-09): the marked question of every guard factory and none for a route without a guard; the session asked before the body is read; the store asked once a request; an answer kept without the CSRF check not serving a call with it; an answer below zero or above one second asked again; a forgotten answer asked again; a refusal not kept.

  - **Prerequisites:** GATE-EKC
  - **Scope:**
    - Paths:
      - `backend/erev_engine/dates.py` (ruling D-79 on SPEC-Q-79):
        - `first_open_period_on_or_after(entity, book_code, d)` returns the earliest period ending on or after `d` whose state for the book is `open`, `closing` or `reopened`, else `None`;
        - `OPEN_STATES` is replaced by `POSTABLE_STATES = frozenset({"open", "closing", "reopened"})`, citing ENGINE_SPEC S08-R-08, 05 RCP-04 and CV-13;
        - a period without a state for the book still raises `ENGINE_INVARIANT_VIOLATED` (CV-13).
      - `backend/erev_engine/progress.py` (ruling D-79 on SPEC-Q-78):
        - `time_fraction` returns 1 for `d ≥ end` only under `DAILY` and `MONTHLY_EVEN`;
        - under `MID_MONTH` it evaluates POLICIES ALG-11 at every `d ≥ start`: (counted periods whose last day ≤ d) ÷ m.
      - `backend/erev_engine/guards.py`: `no_floats` walks `set` and `frozenset` members in the order `sorted(members, key=lambda m: (type(m).__qualname__, repr(m)))`, so the `FLOAT_DETECTED` detail does not depend on `PYTHONHASHSEED` (DG-ENG-02; CV-23).
      - `backend/tests/properties/test_prop_p04_schedule_bounds.py` (ruling D-79: dev-guide §9.7 P4 governs):
        - a module-level check function `p04_bound_violations(exact, allocation, path, minor_unit, cumulative)` returns every f_t < 1 at which |C_t − X × f_t × 10^μ| > 1/2 without a binding bound. For A ≥ 0 a bound binds when C_t = 0 with X × f_t × 10^μ < 0, or C_t = A with X × f_t × 10^μ > A. For A < 0 it binds when C_t = 0 with X × f_t × 10^μ > 0, or C_t = A with X × f_t × 10^μ < A;
        - `test_p04_helper_bounds` asserts that this function returns nothing, in place of the `value not in (0, allocation)` exemption.
      - `backend/tests/architecture/test_engine_purity.py` (ruling D-79 extending D-78 on DG-ARC-02). Under `backend/erev_engine` these are findings:
        - bare-name calls of `eval`, `exec`, `compile`, `globals`, `vars`, `breakpoint` and `input`;
        - any reference to the name `__builtins__`;
        - an attribute `now`, `today`, `utcnow`, `fromtimestamp` or `utcfromtimestamp` on any owner;
        - `getattr(<x>, "now" | "today" | "utcnow")`.

        Attribute calls such as `re.compile(` are not findings, and the module docstring states exactly this list.
      - Tests: `backend/tests/engine/kernel/test_dates.py`, `backend/tests/engine/kernel/test_progress.py`, `backend/tests/engine/kernel/test_guards.py`, `backend/tests/properties/test_prop_p04_schedule_bounds.py`, `backend/tests/architecture/test_engine_purity.py`.

        - Each snippet under `backend/erev_engine/stages/x.py` yields exactly one DG-ARC-02 finding: `x = eval("1")\n`, `exec("x = 1")\n`, `code = compile("1", "x", "eval")\n`, `f = __builtins__["__import__"]\n`, `stamp = as_of.today()\n`, `stamp = datetime.min.now()\n`, `clock = datetime\nstamp = clock.now()\n` and `stamp = getattr(datetime, "now")()\n`.
        - `pattern = re.compile("x")\n` and `from datetime import date as D\nday = D(2026, 9, 12)\n` yield none.

- [ ] **PLF-3b EKC review remediation, part 2: answer-key runner expected problems, the returns basis identity and key selection (ER-C-01, ER-C-02, ER-G-01). Supervisor item.**
  - **Prerequisites:** GATE-EKC; PLF-3a
  - **Scope:**
    - Paths:
      - `backend/tests/support/answer_keys/runners.py`:
        - `expect_problem` (dev-guide §9.5.5; ruling D-79 on SPEC-Q-108):
          - For a timeline item carrying `expect_problem`, `run_engine` computes one step bundle for the item's combination group. The bundle holds the items with `seq` ≤ that item's `seq`, excluding earlier `expect_problem` items.
          - The step passes when `compute` raises `EngineError` whose `code` equals `expect_problem.code`. Otherwise the run records a mismatch with subject `timeline seq <n>`, field `expect_problem`, expected that code, and actual `computed` or the other code.
          - The item never enters a checkpoint bundle or a later step bundle, and the timeline continues.
          - `RunResult` carries each step outcome.
          - The `detail["findings"]` of every expected-problem step with `seq` ≤ `after_seq` joins that checkpoint's `exceptions` comparison as `ERROR` findings (ENGINE_SPEC CV-15, CV-41; dev-guide §9.5.6 `ERROR` ↔ `BLOCKING`).
        - DG-AK-54 identity 1 (ruling D-79 on SPEC-Q-112):
          - The node `tp_allocation_basis:<group>:-` equals Σ obligation `allocated_amount` − `contract_version.expected_returns_amount`. That is Σ `a_posted` under ENGINE_SPEC S04-R-02 and S04-R-08, ENGINE_SPEC_B S09-R-23, and ENGINE_SPEC rev 1.2 B3 decision 2.
          - The mismatch field is `DG-AK-54 sum allocated_amount - expected_returns_amount = tp_allocation_basis`.
          - The V1 and DB-17 checks are unchanged.
      - `backend/tests/support/answer_keys/loader.py`: new `active_selection(environ=None)`, which returns the active keys among `select_keys(load_all(include_withdrawn=True), include_withdrawn=True, **selection_from_env(environ))`. An unknown family, id or REQ still raises `ValueError` (SPEC-Q-106).
      - `backend/tests/answer_keys/test_answer_keys.py`: parametrises over `active_selection()`. A withdrawn id in `ID` is therefore validated and reported, never collected (DG-AK-13; ruling D-79 on SPEC-Q-106).
      - `backend/tests/support/answer_keys/report.py`: when the selection holds no active key, the run appends `{"stage": "select", "exit_code": 1, "message": "the selection holds no active answer key (DG-AK-13)"}`, starts no pytest, and prints `FAIL answer-keys: the selection holds no active answer key (DG-AK-13)`.
      - Tests: `backend/tests/unit/answer_keys/test_runner_engine.py`, `backend/tests/unit/answer_keys/test_assert_checkpoints.py`, `backend/tests/unit/answer_keys/test_loader_validation.py`.
    - Schema: none
    - API: none
    - Engine: none (test harness only; `erev_engine.compute` is still absent, so stub computes installed with `monkeypatch.setattr(erev_engine, "compute", …, raising=False)` drive the tests)

- [ ] **PLF-3c EKC review remediation, part 3: gate and harness integrity for skipped properties, ci overrides, guard wiring and checkpoint-assertion coverage (ER-G-03, ER-G-04, ER-G-05, ER-C-05, ER-G-02). Supervisor item.**
  - **Prerequisites:** GATE-EKC; PLF-3b
  - **Scope:**
    - Paths:
      - `scripts/gate_report.py`: `--fail-on-skipped` may name the target when `--counts-junit` is given. A skipped test in that flat summary fails the run with `{"stage": "skipped", "exit_code": 1, "label": <target>}` and the last line `FAIL <target>: <n> skipped test(s) in <target> (DG-TST-09)`.
      - `Makefile`:
        - the `properties` recipe passes `--fail-on-skipped properties` (ruling D-79 extending D-78: DG-TST-09 and G5 bind `make properties`);
        - the `ci` recipe also passes `--refuse "OVERRIDES=$(strip $(MAKEOVERRIDES))"`, so any command-line variable fails before the first stage, including `PYTEST_PATHS`, `PYTEST_MARKERS` and `RUN_VITEST` (ruling D-79 on DG-MK-ci "Variables: none").
      - `backend/tests/support/answer_keys/runners.py` (ruling D-79; 05 RCP-11; ENGINE_SPEC §0.5):
        - every `BookInput` of a bundle needs a `BookOutput` with the same `book_code`. A missing one, including the checkpoint book, is a mismatch with subject `book <code>`, field `computed` and actual `<absent>`;
        - a `BookOutput` other than `LEGACY` whose `contract_version` is `None` is a mismatch with field `contract_version`.
      - Tests: `backend/tests/unit/test_makefile_targets.py`, `backend/tests/unit/test_controls_report.py`, `backend/tests/unit/answer_keys/test_assert_checkpoints.py`, `backend/tests/unit/answer_keys/test_coverage.py`.

    - Tests:
      - `backend/tests/unit/test_makefile_targets.py::test_g5_skipped_property_test_fails_properties` (ER-G-03):
        - Command: `scripts/gate_report.py properties --command "make properties" --stage "properties=cp <tmp>/three-passed-one-skipped.xml <tmp>/junit.xml" --counts-junit <tmp>/junit.xml --fail-on-skipped properties --reports-dir <tmp>/reports`.
        - It exits 1, and the last line is `FAIL properties: 1 skipped test in properties (DG-TST-09)`. `report.json` has `exit_code` 1, `counts.skipped` 1 and `failures` `[{"stage": "skipped", "exit_code": 1, "label": "properties"}]`.
        - With a JUnit file of 3 passed and 0 skipped, the run ends `OK properties` with `counts.passed` 3.
        - `--fail-on-skipped properties` without `--junit` or `--counts-junit` still exits with the message naming `properties`.
        - The `properties` recipe text passes `--fail-on-skipped properties`, and `test_dg_mk_properties_report_counts` still passes.
      - `backend/tests/unit/test_makefile_targets.py::test_dg_mk_ci_refuses_command_line_overrides` (ER-G-04):
        - Command: `scripts/gate_report.py ci --command "make ci PYTEST_PATHS=backend/tests/engine/kernel/test_money.py" --refuse "TESTS=" --refuse "K=" --refuse "FRONTEND=" --refuse "OVERRIDES=PYTEST_PATHS=backend/tests/engine/kernel/test_money.py" --stage "env=touch <tmp>/ran" --reports-dir <tmp>/reports`.
        - It exits 1 and leaves `<tmp>/ran` absent. `report.json` has `failures` `[{"stage": "variables", "exit_code": 1, "variables": ["OVERRIDES"]}]`.
        - With `--refuse "OVERRIDES="`, the stage runs and the last line is `OK ci`.

        - A check function `root_hook_findings(source)` parses `backend/tests/conftest.py`. It returns `[]` when the `tryfirst` `pytest_collection_modifyitems` hook calls `markers.check(items, TESTS_ROOT)` before `items.sort(...)`.

      - `backend/tests/unit/answer_keys/test_assert_checkpoints.py::test_optional_blocks_compared` (ER-G-02): synthetic keys (in-memory copies) and outputs.
        - Each violation yields exactly one mismatch naming its field:
          - a schedule row in `amounts` form with one wrong period amount (field `amount`);
          - a period-form schedule row with a wrong `amount`, then a wrong `cumulative_amount`, then a wrong `quantity`;
          - a `trace` assertion with a wrong node value (field `value`), then a wrong `formula_id`, then an absent node;
          - a `modifications` row whose `proposed_treatments` differ (field `proposed_treatments`);
          - an `exceptions` row list with one extra `WARNING` diagnostic of a listed code on the checkpoint book (leftover, field `finding`);
          - a `RunResult` missing one checkpoint (subject `checkpoint`, field `run`).
        - The same extra diagnostic on another book gives no mismatch.
        - A two-book output (ASC606 and IFRS15) whose checkpoint book holds the expected figures gives no mismatch. Swapping the two books' figures gives at least one mismatch.

      - `::test_bs1_d_19_step_up_window`: a test-only route guarded by `require_step_up()` returns 200 when `mfa_verified_at` is 4 minutes old and 403 `mfa-step-up-required` when it is 6 minutes old.
      - `::test_regenerate_recovery_codes_invalidates_previous_batch`: `POST /me/recovery-codes` with a fresh step-up returns ten new codes, and a code of the earlier batch fails.

    - Tests:
      - `backend/tests/api/test_files.py::test_req_sec_012_rejects_macro_and_archives`: purpose `IMPORT_SOURCE` uploads of an `.xlsx` containing `xl/vbaProject.bin`, an `.xlsm`, an OLE2 file (`D0 CF 11 E0`) and a `.zip` each return 422 `upload-type-not-allowed` with detail `Import files must be .xlsx or .csv and at most 50 MiB. Macro-enabled workbooks and archives are not accepted.`
      - `::test_upload_limits_per_purpose`: a 26,214,401-byte `ATTACHMENT` returns 422 `upload-type-not-allowed` with detail `Attachments and SSP studies must be pdf, docx, xlsx, csv, png, jpg or eml files of at most 25 MiB.`; a `LEGACY_DATABASE` without the header `SQLite format 3\0` returns detail `Legacy databases must be SQLite files of at most 500 MiB.`; purpose `REPORT_OUTPUT` returns 422 `validation-failed` on field `purpose`.
      - `::test_upl_04_zip_limits`: an OOXML file with one entry compressed at 101:1 returns 422 `upload-type-not-allowed`.

    - Tests:
      - `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_numbering.py::test_krn_num_01_allocation_and_format`: in a provisioned tenant, `next_number(uow, "APPROVAL")` returns `APR-000001` and then `APR-000002`; `next_numbers(uow, "CONTRACT", 3)` returns `CON-000001`, `CON-000002` and `CON-000003`.
      - `::test_krn_num_02_rollback_releases_number`: a unit of work that allocates `APR-000001` and rolls back is followed by an allocation returning `APR-000001`.
      - `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_provisioning.py::test_krn_ten_01_numbering_series`: 13 `numbering_series` rows, one per T-PLT-26 code except `JE`, with prefixes `CON-`, `MOD-`, `APR-`, `IMP-`, `JR-`, `ADJ-`, `REC-`, `RPT-`, `CLS-`, `EXC-`, `EVP-`, `MIG-` and `JDG-`, `next_value` 1, `padding` 6 and `is_gapless` false.
      - `backend/tests/unit/test_jobs_registry.py::test_krn_job_10_job_queue_equals_profile`: `QUEUES` equals `("compute", "imports", "close", "outbox", "reports", "maintenance", "integrations", "ai")`; `JOB_QUEUE` maps the 25 E-14 kinds exactly as DG-KRN-JOB-10 and 05 §5.6.
      - `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_jobs.py::test_krn_job_02_defer_after_commit`: `uow.defer(kind, {})` inserts a `QUEUED` job whose `queue` equals `JOB_QUEUE[kind]`; after commit a Procrastinate job exists with `queueing_lock = str(job id)` and `job.procrastinate_job_id` is set; a rolled-back unit of work defers nothing.
      - `::test_krn_job_03_per_tenant_slots`: with the default `platform.job_concurrency` 4, four `RUNNING` jobs of one tenant hold slots, and a fifth returns to `QUEUED` and is re-deferred with a 5-second delay; a job whose `parent_job_id` names a `RUNNING` job of kind `CLOSE_RUN` runs without taking a slot.
      - `::test_krn_job_06_sweeper_requeues_undispatched_jobs`: a `QUEUED` job with null `procrastinate_job_id` created 61 seconds earlier is re-deferred by the sweeper.
      - Job audit events (header rev 1.37; 04 T-PLT-27 rev 1.106; supervisor rulings R-50 (a), R-62 (a)):
        - `::test_r_50_a_defer_writes_job_start_in_the_deferring_transaction`, `::test_r_50_a_the_terminal_state_writes_job_finish`, `::test_r_50_a_the_scheduler_fan_out_writes_job_start_as_system` and `::test_r_50_a_a_tenant_that_cannot_be_audited_never_stops_the_fan_out` (header rev 1.37; 04 T-PLT-27 rev 1.106; supervisor rulings R-50 (a), R-62 (a)): `defer` buffers `job.start` on the unit of work (its principal and request id; never `params`) and a discarded unit of work leaves neither job nor event; the transaction that records a terminal state writes `job.finish` as SYSTEM under `job-<id>` with the state and the slug of the problem, a retry and a duplicate delivery write nothing; the scheduler fan-out appends `job.start` as SYSTEM under the sweep's request id, a second fan-out defers and writes nothing, and a tenant whose chain cannot take the event gets no job while the tenants after it are served; an `OUTBOX_RELAY` job writes neither event; the chain verifies after each.
        - `backend/tests/unit/test_jobs_registry.py::test_r_62_a_every_kind_is_audited_or_a_transport_kind` and `::test_r_50_a_job_event_payloads_name_no_params` (header rev 1.37): `AUDITED_KINDS` and `TRANSPORT_KINDS` (`OUTBOX_RELAY`, `EMAIL_DELIVERY`, `WEBHOOK_DELIVERY`) partition the 25 E-14 kinds, so a new kind cannot fall outside both.
      - `backend/tests/unit/test_worker.py::test_krn_job_11_queue_option`: `erev worker --queues gpu` exits 2 printing `unknown queue gpu`; without `--queues` and with `EREV_WORKER_QUEUES` empty, `main` receives all eight queues.
      - `::test_krn_job_08_heartbeat_and_check`: the heartbeat writer touches the heartbeat file at 0, 15 and 30 seconds of FrozenClock time; `erev worker --check` exits 0 when the file is younger than 60 seconds and 1 otherwise.
      - `backend/tests/architecture/test_registries_complete.py::test_dg_arc_08_job_registries`: `JOB_QUEUE` covers the 25 E-14 kinds; the registered handlers and `PENDING_JOB_HANDLERS` are disjoint and together equal E-14; every pending entry names its phase code of PHASES §5.3 (for example `("CONTRACT_COMPUTE", "CTR")`).
      - `backend/tests/unit/test_makefile_targets.py::test_plf_worker_target`: `worker` is `.PHONY` and runs `backend/.venv/bin/erev worker` without `--queues`.

    - Tests:
      - `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_outbox.py::test_krn_evt_04_enqueue_dedupe_and_after_commit`: two `enqueue` calls with the same topic and `dedupe_key` insert one row; after commit an `OUTBOX_RELAY` job is deferred; a rolled-back unit of work inserts nothing and defers nothing.
      - `::test_krn_evt_05_relay_backoff_and_dead_letter`: a handler failing once sets `FAILED`, `attempt_count` 1 and `next_attempt_at` = now + 60 seconds; the seventh failure schedules now + 3,600 seconds (cap); the tenth failure sets `DEAD`.
      - `::test_ntr_05_fake_email_file`: relaying the provisioning invitation message writes one `.eml` file under `.run/mail/acme-test/`, holding the link `http://127.0.0.1:5270/accept-invitation#token=` followed by the 43-character token, and no amount.
      - `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_notifications.py::test_ntr_01_identical_notifications_merged`: two `APPROVAL_VOIDED` notifications for one subject within 10 minutes create one row; a third after 11 minutes creates a second row.
      - `::test_krn_evt_06_preferences_respected`: with `email` false for `ITEM_APPROVED`, notifying writes the in-app row and no `EMAIL` message; setting `email = false` for `CHAIN_VERIFICATION_FAILED` violates `ck_notification_preference__mandatory`.
      - `::test_membership_activation_seeds_preferences`: `on_membership_activated` inserts 12 preference rows with `email` true for `APPROVAL_ASSIGNED`, `ITEM_REJECTED`, `APPROVAL_VOIDED`, `JOB_FAILED`, `PERIOD_REOPENED`, `CHAIN_VERIFICATION_FAILED`, `EXPORT_FAILED` and `SUPPORT_GRANT_REQUESTED`, and false for `ITEM_APPROVED`, `PERIOD_LOCKED`, `CLOSE_BLOCKER_RAISED` and `EXCEPTION_ASSIGNED`.
      - `::test_approval_notifications_ntf_01_to_04`: submitting creates `APPROVAL_ASSIGNED` for holders of the step permission other than the preparer, titled `Approval needed: <summary>`, with `link_path` `/approvals/requests/<request id>`; rejecting creates `ITEM_REJECTED` for the preparer; voiding creates `APPROVAL_VOIDED` for the preparer and the assigned approvers.
      - `backend/tests/api/test_me_notifications.py::test_read_all_before`: with three unread notifications created at t1 < t2 < t3, `POST /me/notifications/read-all {"before": t2}` returns `{"marked": 2}`; `GET /me/notifications?unread=true&count=true` returns header `X-Erev-Total-Count: 1`.
      - `::test_notification_preferences_put_and_mandatory_kind`: `PUT /me/notification-preferences` with 12 entries returns 200; an entry turning off email for `CHAIN_VERIFICATION_FAILED` returns 422 `validation-failed`.
      - `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_provisioning.py::test_krn_ten_01_invitation_outbox`: a provisioned tenant has one `outbox_message` of topic `EMAIL` whose payload holds the invitation link and no `invitation_token_sha256` value.
      - `backend/tests/architecture/test_registries_complete.py::test_dg_arc_08_outbox_handlers`: `HANDLERS` and `PENDING_OUTBOX_HANDLERS` are disjoint and together equal the 4 E-70 values; `JOURNAL_EXPORT` is pending with `CLO`, `SYNC_REQUEST` with `DIN` and `WEBHOOK` with `PLF`.
      - `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_notification_scope_db.py::test_an_event_is_told_to_the_holders_whose_role_covers_it` and `::test_a_monitors_item_is_told_to_the_revenue_accountants_of_its_entity`, `backend/tests/domain/journals/test_export_failure_recipients.py::test_a_failed_export_is_told_to_the_controllers_of_the_batchs_entity` and `backend/tests/unit/events/test_role_holders_scope.py` (item NOTIFY-ROLE-HOLDERS-SCOPE-1; PRD rev 1.175; 05 NTR-02 rev 1.180): a notification addressed by role goes to the holders whose role covers the event — the lock and the reopen of a period, a monitor's item and a failed export to the holders for the entity or for all entities; the chain's failed verification and an operator's request for access to the holders for all entities alone; the eight calls of `role_holders` name their scope
      - `backend/tests/domain/close/test_close_run_failure_recipients_db.py::test_a_failed_close_run_is_told_to_the_delegates_whose_own_scope_covers_its_entity` and `::test_the_permissions_reader_answers_by_the_scope_of_the_subject` (item NOTIFY-DELEGATE-SCOPE-1; dev-guide DG-KRN-EVT-06 rev 1.250): a failed close run of AVM-US is told to its initiator, to the holder of `period.lock` for all entities and to the two of that holder's three delegates whose own entity scope covers AVM-US; a holder of the permission for AVM-DE alone and a delegate of AVM-DE alone are not told; asked without an entity, `permission_holders` answers the holders and the delegates of all entities

- [ ] **PLF-15 Invitation acceptance, password reset and password change.**
  - **Prerequisites:** PLF-14
  - **Scope:**
    - Paths: `backend/erev_api/db/migrations/versions/0017_password_reset_token.py`; `backend/erev_api/db/tables/platform.py` (`password_reset_token`); `backend/erev_api/api/v1/session.py` (`POST /session/accept-invitation`, `POST /session/invitations/lookup`, `POST /session/password-reset`, `POST /session/password-reset/confirm`); `backend/erev_api/api/v1/me.py` (`POST /me/password`); `backend/erev_api/domain/platform/memberships.py` (`accept_invitation`); `backend/tests/api/test_invitations.py`; `backend/tests/api/test_password_reset.py`; `backend/tests/api/test_me_password.py`; `docs/api/openapi.json`; `frontend/src/lib/api/schema.d.ts`
    - Schema: T-PLT-42 `password_reset_token` (IM-E, RLS-NONE-U; column grants of 04 §14.2)
    - API: API-R-01 `POST /session/accept-invitation`, `POST /session/invitations/lookup`, `POST /session/password-reset`, `POST /session/password-reset/confirm`; API-R-03 `POST /me/password`
    - Engine: none
    - Screens: none
  - **Acceptance:**
    - Tests:
      - `backend/tests/api/test_invitations.py::test_lookup_and_accept`: with the token of the provisioning email, `POST /session/invitations/lookup {token}` returns 200 `{workspace_display_name: "Acme Test", inviter_display_name: null, email: "admin@acme.test", expires_at}` (null because `created_by_kind` is `OPERATOR`); `POST /session/accept-invitation {token, password: "password1234"}` returns 422 `password-policy` with detail `Choose a less common password.`; with `Lena!Revenue2026` it returns 200 with the session active in the tenant; the membership becomes `ACTIVE` with `activated_at` set and both invitation columns null; `TENANT_SELECTED` and the audit event `membership.accept` are written; 12 notification preferences exist.
      - `::test_expired_or_unknown_token_not_found`: after the clock advances 7 days and 1 second, lookup returns 404 `not-found`; an unknown token also returns 404.
      - `backend/tests/api/test_password_reset.py::test_request_always_202`: `POST /session/password-reset {email}` returns 202 with an empty body for an unknown email (no token, no email) and for a known active user (one token expiring in 60 minutes, `PASSWORD_RESET_REQUESTED`, one `EMAIL` message with a link containing `/password/reset/confirm#token=`); the sixth request for one user within an hour returns 202, writes `PASSWORD_RESET_REQUESTED` with outcome `DENIED` and sends nothing.
      - `backend/tests/api/test_password_reset.py::test_reset_email_open_invitation_1_an_invited_person_resets_and_accepts`: a person removed from her workspace and invited to another asks for a reset — the email is written in the inviting workspace, the security event names it — sets a new password and accepts the invitation with it (rev 1.39; 04 T-PLT-42 rule 1 rev 1.308)
      - `backend/tests/api/test_password_reset.py::test_reset_email_open_invitation_1_who_gets_no_email_and_who_gets_it_elsewhere`: a member is answered in her own workspace whatever invitation stands beside it; an expired invitation and a disabled identity get no email; of several invitations the one sent last, a tie by tenant id; the limit of five requests an hour holds (rev 1.39)
      - `backend/tests/api/test_password_reset.py::test_reset_email_open_invitation_1_no_invitation_is_without_a_token_hash`: an `INVITED` membership whose token hash is null — with its expiry or without — is refused by T-PLT-07's two checks, so an invitation nobody can accept is never the workspace of the email (rev 1.39)
      - `backend/tests/api/test_users_api.py::test_reset_email_open_invitation_1_a_person_invited_again_resets_and_accepts`: the second life in ONE workspace — a member removed and invited again who forgot the first life's password resets it by the email written in that workspace and accepts with the new one; invited and never accepted she gets neither token nor email, removed and not invited a token and no email (rev 1.39)
      - `::test_confirm_resets_and_ends_sessions`: `POST /session/password-reset/confirm {token, new_password}` returns 204; every session of the user ends with `PASSWORD_CHANGED`; `PASSWORD_RESET_COMPLETED` and `PASSWORD_CHANGED` are written; reusing the token returns 404 `not-found`; a weak password returns 422 `password-policy`.
      - `backend/tests/api/test_me_password.py::test_change_password`: a wrong current password returns 422 `validation-failed` with `errors[0].field == "current_password"`; success returns 204, ends the user's other sessions with `PASSWORD_CHANGED`, keeps the current session and writes `PASSWORD_CHANGED`.

- [ ] **PLF-16 Approvals and delegations API.**
  - **Prerequisites:** PLF-15
  - **Scope:**
    - Paths: `backend/erev_api/api/v1/approvals.py`; `backend/erev_api/schemas/approvals.py` (API-S-Approval and the command schemas of 04 §16.10); `backend/erev_api/domain/platform/approval_queries.py`; `backend/erev_api/api/lists.py` (`ListSpec`, `list_params`, `paginate`, cursor encoding, `count=true`); BUILD_SPEC header 1.139 (item APR-CONTENT-SCOPE-1): `approval_queries.content_visible`, `content_readable`, `shown` and `amount_key`, `backend/erev_api/approvals/subjects.py` (`SUBJECT_LABELS`, `withheld_summary`), `backend/erev_api/domain/platform/file_access.py` (`request_content_visible`), `backend/erev_api/domain/platform/attachments.py` (`preview_refusal`), `backend/erev_api/approvals/engine.py` (`_preparer_summary`); `backend/tests/api/test_approvals_api.py`; `backend/tests/unit/test_lists.py`; `docs/api/openapi.json`; `frontend/src/lib/api/schema.d.ts`; rev 1.14: `backend/erev_api/domain/platform/approval_delegations.py` (the delegation commands); `backend/erev_api/approvals/delegations.py` (the end of a delegation that lost its support); `backend/erev_api/auth/sod.py` and `backend/erev_api/auth/permissions.py` (`standing_delegations`); `backend/tests/api/test_delegation_limits.py`; `backend/tests/architecture/test_delegation_end.py`
    - Schema: none
    - API: API-R-09 `GET /approvals`, `GET /approvals/{id}`, `POST /approvals/{id}/approve`, `/reject`, `/withdraw`, `POST /approvals/bulk-approve`, `GET, POST /approval-delegations`, `POST /approval-delegations/{id}/revoke`
    - Engine: none
    - Screens: none
  - **Acceptance:**
    - Tests:
      - `backend/tests/api/test_approvals_api.py::test_list_filters_and_sort`: `GET /approvals?assigned_to_me=true&status=PENDING&sort=submitted_at` returns only requests whose active step permission the caller holds and did not prepare, oldest first; `preparer=me` returns the caller's requests; `sort=amount` orders largest amount first; `count=true` sets `X-Erev-Total-Count`.
      - `::test_approval_detail_and_can_decide`: `GET /approvals/{id}` returns every API-S-Approval field of 04 §16.10 and an `ETag`; `can_decide` is false for the preparer and true for another holder of the step permission.
      - `::test_approve_needs_hash_and_step_up`: `POST /approvals/{id}/approve` without `subject_content_sha256` returns 422 `validation-failed`; with the hash and a step-up older than 5 minutes it returns 403 `mfa-step-up-required`; with a fresh step-up it returns 200 with status `APPROVED`.
      - `::test_reject_requires_comment`: `POST /approvals/{id}/reject {}` returns 422 `validation-failed` on field `comment`.
      - `::test_bulk_approve_bounds`: 201 items return 422 `validation-failed` on field `items`; two items return 200 with two per-item results.
      - `::test_delegations`: `POST /approval-delegations` naming a permission the delegator does not hold returns 422 `validation-failed`; a valid delegation returns 201; `POST /approval-delegations/{id}/revoke` sets `revoked_at`.
      - Rev 1.14 (PRD BR-PLT-07 rev 1.118; 04 T-PLT-21 rev 1.189; supervisor rulings R-111 (2), (4), (6) and (9)), `backend/tests/api/test_delegation_limits.py`: `::test_r_111_2_a_delegation_goes_only_to_a_member_with_a_confirmed_second_factor`: a delegate without a confirmed second factor returns 422 `validation-failed` on `delegate_membership_id` with rule `BR-PLT-07`, also after an enrolment that was started and not confirmed; a delegate with an authenticator app returns 201.
      - `::test_r_111_4_a_delegation_ends_within_90_days_of_the_command`: a `valid_to` later than 90 days after the command returns 422 on `valid_to` ("A delegation ends within 90 days of today.") whatever `valid_from` is, and a following row for the next 90 days is refused the same way.
      - `::test_r_111_4_a_delegation_is_checked_for_separation_of_duties`: delegating `import.approve` and `contract.approve` to a Revenue Accountant returns 409 `sod-conflict` with `errors[].field` `permissions[0]` and rule `SoD-3`; under an approved exception it returns 201.
      - `::test_r_111_4_a_role_is_checked_against_a_delegation_that_has_not_started` and `::test_r_111_4_a_delegation_is_checked_against_one_that_has_not_started`: a delegation given for a later start counts at a command from the moment it is given — the conflicting role returns 409 `sod-conflict` (`SoD-3` on `role_id`) and a second delegation 409 (`SoD-1` on `permissions[0]`); once the first is revoked the role returns 201.
      - `::test_r_111_4_a_delegation_ends_when_its_delegator_loses_the_permission_or_the_membership` and `::test_r_111_4_a_grant_revives_no_delegation`: a revoked role assignment, a suspended membership and a changed role each set `revoked_at` on the delegations they leave unsupported, with the audit action `approval_delegation.end` and its `detail.cause`; the role returning gives nothing back.
      - `::test_r_111_4_an_access_administrator_ends_any_delegation`: a holder of `role.manage` lists the delegations they may end — every delegation for a holder for all entities (rev 1.26) — and ends one whose delegator's access lies within their own, with the step-up; beyond it 403 `forbidden` with rule `T-PLT-10`; a stale step-up 403 `mfa-step-up-required`; each refusal is a `DENIED` event; a member who is no party is answered 404 and the delegate 403.
      - `::test_deleg_list_scope_1_an_administrator_of_named_entities_lists_what_they_may_end` (rev 1.26; item DELEG-LIST-SCOPE-1; 04 T-PLT-21 rev 1.261; ruling R-28): two administrators of one entity each list the delegation of their own entity's reviewer and the one given to one of them — never the delegation of a member with a role in each entity or of a reviewer of all entities, which the administrator of every entity lists; what an administrator lists and did not receive they end, the one received from a delegator beyond their entities answers 403 `forbidden` with rule `T-PLT-10`; once the role beyond an administrator's entities is revoked she lists that delegation and ends it.
      - `::test_r_111_9_the_findings_of_a_delegation`: the six findings of one request are returned together in field order; a suspended member and a member of another workspace are no delegate; a permission held through a delegation is not delegated on.
      - `backend/tests/architecture/test_delegation_end.py::test_dg_krn_apr_09_every_writer_of_access_ends_what_it_leaves_unsupported`: every function that writes `role_assignment`, `role_permission` or `tenant_membership` calls the sweep or is listed with its reason.
      - `::test_apr_content_scope_a_reader_of_one_entity_is_answered_the_header_of_a_request` (BUILD_SPEC header 1.139; item APR-CONTENT-SCOPE-1; 04 §16.10 rev 1.208 "Content of a request"): a reader who covers one entity of a request of two is answered the header and `content_withheld` — `summary` and `subject.display` read the subject type's name and the request's number, `amount` and `impact_preview` are null, `flags` and `attachments` empty, no decision carries its comment, and nothing in the answer names the other entity; the preview file and an attached file answer 404 on `GET /files/{id}` and `/content`, `GET /attachments` lists nothing and `POST /attachments` answers 404; an approval keeps 403 `forbidden`, alone and in bulk; `sort=amount` orders the withheld row last, on one page and across pages, with the same count; `q` and an amount range answer 422 for every reader. Beside each, the reader who covers both entities and the preparer read everything.
      - `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_approval_scope.py::test_content_is_shown_to_who_covers_every_entity_and_to_nobody_else` (header 1.139): the predicate is the listing rule with "every" for "at least one" — an approver for one entity, a member who reads both entities and approves for one, and the delegate of an approver for one are answered the header; an approver for both, the preparer and a delegate of an approver for both, the content; a decider who lost an entity, the header. `::test_the_content_rule_is_the_listing_rule_but_for_a_request_of_several_entities`: for a request of no entity, of one and of all entities whoever lists it reads its content. `::test_a_preparer_is_shown_and_told_its_request_as_its_scope_reads_it`: a preparer who covers part of its own request is answered the header, and `ITEM_APPROVED`, `ITEM_REJECTED` and `APPROVAL_VOIDED` tell it the withheld summary, the rejection without the decider's comment.
      - `backend/tests/domain/imports/test_entity_scope.py::test_apr_content_scope_an_approver_for_one_entity_of_an_import_reads_none_of_its_rows` (header 1.139): the finding through the product — an approver of imports for one entity of an upload that names two is answered the header, no preview, and 404 on the preview file.
      - `backend/tests/unit/approvals/test_content_scope.py::test_every_subject_type_has_the_name_the_web_shows`, `::test_the_withheld_summary_names_the_type_and_the_request_only`, `::test_the_list_reads_no_content_column_but_through_the_readers_own_key` (header 1.139): one label per value of E-08, equal to the web's; the catalogue of `GET /approvals` names no content column but the sort key `amount`, which the route replaces by the reader's own.
      - Rev 1.25 (BUILD_SPEC header 1.196; item APR-REQUEST-REASON-1; 04 §16.10 rev 1.252), `backend/tests/api/test_approvals_api.py::test_apr_request_reason_a_request_answers_what_it_was_submitted_with`: API-S-Approval answers the `reason_code` and the `comment` a request was submitted with to a reader of the whole request and null to a reader of its header, who is told neither; `GET /approvals` answers each reader the same row as the single read; a request submitted with neither answers null. `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_approval_scope.py::test_a_request_answers_what_it_was_submitted_with_to_its_readers_and_its_preparer`: the preparer who covers one entity of her request of two reads the two she wrote in an answer that is still a header, an approver for that entity reads neither, and a decision's comment moves neither. `backend/tests/domain/imports/test_entity_scope.py::test_apr_request_reason_a_quarantine_uploader_reads_the_comment_she_submitted_with`: the same through the product — the uploader of an import in quarantine mode reads her comment on a request that names an entity she does not cover, the approver of both entities reads it with the whole request, the approver of one does not. item APR-DECISION-CODE-CONTENT-1, `backend/tests/api/test_approvals_api.py::test_apr_decision_code_content_a_decisions_reason_code_is_withheld_with_its_comment`: the reason code an approver sends with a rejection or an approval is answered to the readers of the whole request and null to a reader of its header, whose answer does not hold its text; `::test_a_preparer_is_shown_and_told_its_request_as_its_scope_reads_it` of the kernel and `::test_apr_rejection_reason_a_quarantine_uploader_is_told_why_her_import_was_rejected` of the imports hold that the preparer reads the code of a rejection with its comment, and the code of an approval not at all.
      - Rev 1.28 (BUILD_SPEC header 1.214; item APR-DENIED-AUDIT-1; 04 §16.10 rev 1.266), `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_approval_scope.py::test_a_decision_the_kernel_refuses_is_on_record_as_denied`: a decision refused by one of the kernel's own checks leaves one `DENIED` audit event on the request with the step's permission and number, the subject and the reason — `NO_AUTHORITY`, `ENTITIES_NOT_COVERED` for an approval and for a rejection, `STEP_ROLE` with the role's code, `SELF_APPROVAL`, `EXCLUDED_DECIDER` with the delegator — and stays when the refusal rolls the command back; a member who cannot read the request is answered 404 and leaves none; the approver who may decide decides and leaves none. `::test_the_other_refusals_of_a_decision_and_of_a_withdrawal_are_on_record`: `mfa-required` of a session without a verified second factor; `approval_request.withdraw` with `NOT_PREPARER` for a withdrawal by a reader of the request and by a caller of the subject's own command, and no event for one who cannot read it; `NOT_A_PERSON` with the id as sent, the same event for an id that names a request and one that names none; a bulk approval records the item it refuses and not the item it decides; and no column of these events holds the request's summary or the comment and the reason code sent with a refused decision. `backend/tests/api/test_approvals_api.py::test_apr_denied_audit_a_stale_step_up_is_on_record_with_the_id_as_sent`: an approval whose step-up is older than five minutes is recorded with the id as sent, the same for a request and for an id that names none, and a bulk approval refused as a whole is one event with the ids as sent; nothing is decided. `::test_a_caller_that_is_no_person_learns_nothing_from_a_decision_route`: the four attempts of an API client to reject and to approve are recorded under the client's own id, the same for the request and for an id that names none. `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_approval_engine.py::test_withdraw_preparer_only` (PLF-11) lists the refused withdrawal as `DENIED` between the submission and the preparer's own.
      - `backend/tests/unit/test_lists.py::test_dg_lst_03_cursor_bound_to_sort_and_filters`: a cursor issued for `sort=-submitted_at` used with `sort=submitted_at` returns 422 `validation-failed` with `rule_id` `API-C-09`; `limit=501` returns 422; `count=true` above 100,000 rows yields `100000+`.

      - `::test_suspend_ends_sessions`: `POST /users/{id}/suspend {reason}` returns 200; the membership is `SUSPENDED`; the user's sessions in the tenant end `REVOKED`; the user's next request returns 401 `unauthenticated`.
      - `::test_reset_mfa_step_up_and_sessions`: without a fresh step-up the command returns 403 `mfa-step-up-required`; with it the factor gets `disabled_at`, `MFA_RESET` is written and the user's sessions end.
      - `::test_resend_invitation_replaces_token`: the token hash changes and `invitation_expires_at` becomes now + 7 days.
      - `::test_viewer_forbidden_and_denied_audit`: a `viewer` calling `POST /users` receives 403 `forbidden`, and one `DENIED` audit event is written.
      - The members of a workspace beside its copies (fragment rev 1.29; item USERS-MEMBER-TENANT-1 — PRODUCT DEFECT; 04 §1.4 RLS-TM rev 1.272): `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_sandbox_reset.py::test_the_members_of_a_workspace_are_its_own_beside_its_copies` — production with four members, a loaded copy and an EMPTY sandbox: `GET /users` lists each member once in production and in the copy, for a Tenant Admin of both and for the requester of the sandboxes, and the requester alone in the EMPTY sandbox; `GET /users/{id}` of the caller's own membership answers 200 in production and in the copy, and 404 `not-found` for a membership of another workspace, asked either way; an access review started in the EMPTY sandbox takes one item. `::test_a_member_of_a_workspace_and_its_copy_is_one_member_in_each` — in production and, the same, from inside the copy: `POST /role-assignments` and `POST /sod-exceptions` for the caller's own membership answer 201, `GET /approvals` with it as `preparer` 200 with the request, and `POST /access-reviews/{id}/start` 200 with an item for each of the four members. `::test_a_member_of_a_workspace_and_its_copy_is_told_once` — such a member, who takes the email of an approval and not its in-app notice, is sent one email for an approval made in that member's own request.
      - A person removed once is invited again (fragment rev 1.37; item SBX-COPY-OPEN-INVITATION-1, its second half; PRD SM-13 rev 1.201; 04 T-PLT-07 rev 1.293): `::test_a_member_removed_after_acceptance_is_invited_again_on_the_same_membership` — 201 with the same membership, `INVITED` with a new token and expiry, `removed_at` null, `invited_at` and `activated_at` as they were; the role of before revoked by the removal and the new one a new row; one trail (invite, accept, remove, invite); 422 for a person who is a member; the second acceptance keeps the first `activated_at`. `::test_an_invitation_withdrawn_before_acceptance_is_issued_again` — the same for a person who never accepted: the withdrawn link is dead, the new one opens, the name can still be corrected, the acceptance is the first. `::test_the_setup_rule_reads_a_past_approver_the_same_after_that_person_is_invited_again` — `routing.second_approver_has_existed` answers True before the removal of the second Tenant Admin, after it, after the new invitation and after its acceptance, and the setup rule approves nothing again. `::test_a_role_asked_for_the_first_life_is_not_decided_for_the_second` — a role request still pending at the withdrawal is voided `STALE_SUBJECT` by the new invitation, cannot be approved, and the approver decides the role of the second invitation alone. `::test_what_an_invitation_ends_does_not_depend_on_the_inviters_entities` — a Tenant Admin for AVM-DE alone invites again a person whose withdrawn invitation left a Controller request for AVM-US pending: the request is voided `STALE_SUBJECT` all the same, cannot be approved, and the inviter is answered the one role it may read. `::test_a_delegation_given_to_the_first_life_does_not_decide_for_the_second` (PRD BR-PLT-07 rev 1.201; 04 T-PLT-21 rev 1.293) — the removal of a delegate ends the delegation, stamped by the remover, with `approval_delegation.end` and `detail.delegate_status`; invited again and active, the person is honoured no delegation and is answered 404 for a request the delegator could decide. `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_privacy_commands.py::test_an_erased_person_is_not_invited_again_on_the_erased_membership` — the invitation of the address an erasure left answers 422 and the membership stays `REMOVED`; the person's own address makes a new identity and a new membership.
      - What stands on a removed row, and two invitations at once (fragment rev 1.38; item INVITE-ENDS-STANDING-ASSIGNMENTS-1; 04 T-PLT-07 rev 1.306): `::test_an_assignment_left_on_a_removed_row_is_revoked_by_the_invitation` — a Controller assignment written on a removed member's row is revoked by the invitation of that person as a Viewer, with its `role_assignment.revoke` event and the inviter's stamp; the answer lists it `REVOKED`, and after the approval and the acceptance the person holds Viewer alone. `::test_two_invitations_of_one_removed_person_are_one` (test only) — two administrators invite the same removed person at once: the second is observed waiting at the membership's row and answers 422, with one invitation event, one email and one role request.
      - A person only invited is named by no reader (fragment rev 1.41; item IDENTITY-BEFORE-ACCEPTANCE-READERS-1; 04 T-PLT-02 rev 1.315; dev-guide DG-KRN-DB-13): `::test_a_person_only_invited_is_named_by_no_reader_before_acceptance` — workspace B invites a person under a name it types, the person accepts and signs in there, and workspace A invites the address. Through the routes alone, before the acceptance A reads the email and no last sign-in: in API-S-User, in the summaries of the invitation's role request, of a role asked for later and of an SoD exception, in the role assignments and the answer for a pending one, in the SoD exception, in the label of the membership's audit events, and in an access review's item and snapshot file. After the acceptance the readers that read when they are asked show the name, and what was written before keeps its words. With it `backend/tests/architecture/test_shown_identity.py::test_dg_krn_db_13_the_person_of_a_membership_is_read_through_the_rule` — a function that reads `app_user.display_name` or `last_login_at` itself is listed with its reason or fails — and `::test_the_scan_sees_a_reader_past_the_rule`.
      - Who is not a member now is shown as one only invited (fragment rev 1.42; item IDENTITY-WITHHELD-BY-STATUS-1; 04 T-PLT-02 rev 1.316; dev-guide DG-KRN-DB-13): `::test_who_is_not_a_member_now_is_shown_as_one_only_invited` — two people pass through workspace A, one whom another workspace named and who signs in there, one whom A names; through the routes alone, an `INVITED` and a `REMOVED` row give no last sign-in and no MFA state and say so (`sign_in_withheld`), the name is the email unless A's invitation created the identity, a sign-in made elsewhere after the removal reaches no reader, `q` does not find the removed member by the other workspace's name, and her role assignment, her audit events and an ended delegation name her by her address. With it `backend/tests/unit/test_actors.py::test_member_names_the_person_as_the_workspace_is_shown_them`, and the lists of `backend/tests/architecture/test_shown_identity.py`: the owner of an exception item and a delegation's parties take the rule, RPT-25's delegator is listed with its reason. An erased identity is not withheld (05 PRV-07 a): `::test_an_erased_person_reads_by_the_erasure_label_whoever_created_the_identity` — erased through the routes by a workspace that had not created the identity, the person reads `Erased user <8 hex>` there as in the workspace that had — with `backend/tests/unit/test_privacy_anonymise_names.py::test_the_rule_knows_an_erased_identity_by_the_address_the_erasure_writes`.

- [ ] **PLF-22 Jobs API, stalled-job monitoring and retention sweep.**
  - **Prerequisites:** PLF-21
  - **Scope:**
    - Paths: `backend/erev_api/api/v1/jobs.py`; `backend/erev_api/schemas/common.py` (API-S-Job serialisation); `backend/erev_api/jobs/sweeper.py` (stall detection with problem `job-stalled`, DG-KRN-JOB-06 second part and 05 JOB-06; periodic task `stalled_job_sweeper`, 05 SCH-04); `backend/erev_api/jobs/registry.py` (final-attempt failure with RFC 9457 `problem` and notification `JOB_FAILED`, DG-KRN-JOB-05; fail-closed exception item per BS1-D-13); `backend/erev_api/domain/platform/retention.py` (`RETENTION_SWEEP` handler removed from `PENDING_JOB_HANDLERS`: deletes `idempotency_record` past `expires_at`, `user_session` past `ended_at` + 30 days, `password_reset_token` past `expires_at`; periodic tasks `idempotency_purge` and `session_purge`, 05 SCH-07 and SCH-08); `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_job_monitoring.py`; `backend/tests/api/test_jobs_api.py`; `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_retention.py`; `docs/api/openapi.json`; `frontend/src/lib/api/schema.d.ts`

    - API: API-R-11 `GET /jobs`, `GET /jobs/{id}`, `POST /jobs/{id}/cancel`
    - Engine: none
    - Screens: none
  - **Acceptance:**
    - Tests:
      - `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_job_monitoring.py::test_ctl_040_stalled_job_failed_and_notified`: a `RUNNING` job whose `updated_at` is 10 minutes 1 second old is set `FAILED` by the sweeper with problem slug `job-stalled` and detail `no heartbeat for 10 minutes`; with `RetryPolicy(max_attempts=3)` it is re-queued twice; after the third failure the job stays `FAILED` and the initiator receives a `JOB_FAILED` notification titled `Job failed: <job label>`.
        - Witnesses of item JOB-FAILED-ITEM-1 (header rev 1.158, fragment rev 1.18; supervisor ruling R-121 (n), from finding F-2 of lane ACCT's control inventory; the supervisor's rulings of 2026-10-01 on the lane's pre-build line and on its correction of the item's severity; 03 rev 1.135, 04 rev 1.226, 05 rev 1.165, dev-guide rev 1.212, PRD rev 1.157; sub-bullets, so the item's criteria count is unchanged):
          - `::test_job_failed_item_1_who_learns_of_a_failed_job`: the same failure of a job started by the scheduler, by an API client and by a user — the log line `operator_alert.raised` of kind `JOB_FAILED` with its values-free fields for the first two and none for the third, the notification for the third alone, and `job.finish` for all three; `::test_job_failed_item_1_an_alert_that_cannot_be_raised_changes_nothing`.
          - `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_job_failed_items.py::test_job_failed_item_1_a_failed_job_of_an_entity_leaves_its_item`: a failed computation of a contract leaves one open `INFO` item `JOB_FAILED` of source `ENGINE` in the transaction that ends the job, naming the entity and the contract; a second failure counts it again; a preview and a job without a subject raise none; a later job of the kind and record that runs to its end resolves it as the system; a failure after that raises a new item.
          - `::test_job_failed_item_1_the_item_holds_no_close_and_is_dismissed_with_a_comment`: the count behind `blockers.exceptions_open` and the gate `EXCEPTIONS_CLEARED` leaves the open item out, where it counts a `WARNING` item of the same entity; `DISMISS` is among its actions and the command closes it with the comment.
          - `::test_job_failed_item_1_an_item_that_cannot_be_raised_leaves_the_job_failed` and `::test_job_failed_item_1_a_failure_hook_that_fails_leaves_nothing_behind`: a reader, a writer or a hook that raises is logged, leaves neither a row nor an audit event of a row that was rolled back, and the job ends `FAILED` with its `job.finish`.
          - `backend/tests/domain/imports/test_commit_job_failed.py::test_job_failed_item_1_a_stalled_commit_ends_its_upload_failed` and `backend/tests/domain/ssp/test_calculator_job_failed.py::test_job_failed_item_1_a_stalled_run_ends_failed`: an upload left `COMMITTING` and an SSP calculator run left `RUNNING` by a worker that died are ended `FAILED` by the kind's failure hook when the sweeper settles the job; a record that ended or that another transaction holds is left alone.
          - `backend/tests/pg/test_job_failed_readers.py::test_job_failed_item_1_the_readers_name_one_entity_or_none` (each reader on rows of its kind's subject) and `::test_job_failed_item_1_the_read_of_a_succeeding_job_is_one_probe_of_the_open_index`; `backend/tests/unit/test_job_failed_kinds.py::test_job_failed_item_1_the_kinds_that_leave_an_item_are_a_closed_list`.
        - Witnesses of item JOB-STALL-COMMIT-RACE-1 (header rev 1.233, fragment rev 1.34; found by lane OPS on 2026-10-02 with the commit of an import; the supervisor's ruling of 2026-10-02 on the lane's pre-build line; 05 rev 1.200, dev-guide rev 1.266, PRD rev 1.191; sub-bullets, so the item's criteria count is unchanged). The stalled notification of `test_ctl_040_stalled_job_failed_and_notified` reads "… no heartbeat for 10 minutes. The job was stopped; what it had not committed by then is not kept.":
          - `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_job_stall_rule.py::test_job_06_a_job_with_a_transaction_open_is_passed_over_and_ends_well` (three cases): a handler ten minutes and a second inside one transaction is passed over at every pass of the sweeper — `job.sweep_passed_over` with the job, its kind, the attempt and 601, then 661 seconds — its work commits and the job ends `SUCCEEDED` under the task that started it; on a last attempt, with attempts left (no second task is dispatched), and where the unit of work holds the job's row as well.
          - `::test_job_06_a_unit_of_work_of_a_stopped_job_is_refused_at_its_beginning`: silent outside a transaction, the job is stopped; the unit of work its handler opens next is refused before its work, the registry logs `job.attempt_lost` and records nothing, and the member reads the stopped job's body. `::test_job_06_a_commit_under_a_job_that_was_moved_keeps_nothing`: a job moved out of `RUNNING` beside an open unit of work — the commit finds no row for its heartbeat, and a handler that takes the refusal and leaves its block commits nothing by that.
          - `::test_job_04_a_beat_of_a_lost_attempt_is_refused_and_leaves_its_successors_row`: after the sweeper handed the job to a second attempt, the first attempt's heartbeat and progress raise and leave the second attempt's row as it is.
          - `::test_job_06_a_unit_that_begins_during_a_settlement_waits_and_is_refused`: a settlement held open in its failure hook; a unit of work of the attempt is read waiting for the job's lock in `pg_stat_activity`, blocked by the settlement's backend, and is refused once the settlement has committed.
          - `::test_dg_krn_db_08_a_jobs_unit_of_work_takes_its_locks_in_one_order`: read from the statements sent — the lock first, the read of the job's row second, the heartbeat directly before the audit chain's head. `::test_job_06_the_heartbeat_takes_no_key_of_the_job_row`: beside an open heartbeat a `FOR KEY SHARE NOWAIT` of the job's row is granted and a job under that parent is inserted and committed; the catalogue's list of the tables that refer to `job` is the eleven the dev guide names.
          - `::test_job_06_a_transaction_in_another_workspace_holds_the_lock_and_beats_first`: a unit of work in another workspace takes the lock and then writes the heartbeat in the job's workspace; the job is passed over while it is open, and once the job is stopped the next such unit and a session that enlists are refused before their work.
          - `::test_rb_07_an_operator_ends_the_transaction_of_a_job_that_is_passed_over`: the statement of runbook RB-07, taken from the runbook's text, names the backend of the job's open unit of work and no other; after `pg_terminate_backend` nothing of the transaction is kept and the job ends `FAILED` as after any error.
          - `::test_job_06_a_transport_job_rests_on_its_messages_claim`: the registered handler of `EMAIL_DELIVERY` through the worker, with a send that takes ten minutes outside a transaction — the job is handed to a second attempt, the message is recorded `DISPATCHED` once under its own claim, the first attempt stops at its beat and the second finds nothing due.
          - `backend/tests/domain/imports/test_commit_job_failed.py::test_job_06_a_commit_in_flight_keeps_its_job_from_being_stopped`: the measured case turned round — a unit of work that holds the upload's row keeps the commit job from being stopped, and ends the upload `COMMITTED` under the job that is still its own.
          - `backend/tests/unit/test_job_unit_doors.py::test_dg_krn_job_04_a_transaction_of_a_job_passes_the_door_or_is_listed` with `::test_dg_krn_job_04_the_scan_tells_a_door_from_a_bare_transaction`: every transaction that can write and that a function handed a job context or a job runtime opens beside its units of work calls `enlist` first or stands in the closed list; `::test_dg_krn_job_04_an_attempt_and_a_context_with_a_job_row_are_the_registrys`; `::test_dg_krn_db_08_the_jobs_lock_is_a_read_a_copy_step_may_send`; `::test_ntf_05_the_body_of_a_stopped_job_says_what_was_not_kept`.
          - `backend/tests/unit/test_uow.py::test_krn_uow_02_before_commit_steps_and_a_commit_that_fails`: the steps run before the commit in their order, and a commit that fails at a step or at the chain leaves no transaction open; `backend/tests/unit/docs/test_guides.py::test_runbook_tells_a_job_that_is_passed_over_and_what_ends_it`.
          - `backend/tests/pg/test_rls_index_plans.py::test_a_jobs_unit_of_work_reaches_its_job_through_the_primary_key`: the read of the job's row where a unit of work begins and the heartbeat at its commit are each one probe of the primary key, with half of the tenant's three hundred jobs live.
      - `::test_krn_job_05_last_attempt_problem_stored`: the final `job.problem` is an RFC 9457 object with `type` `https://erev.dev/problems/job-stalled`.
      - `backend/tests/api/test_jobs_api.py::test_api_s_job_shape_and_access`: `GET /jobs/{id}` returns `id`, `kind`, `state`, `progress {done, total}`, `result`, `problem`, `created_by`, `created_at`, `started_at` and `finished_at`; another user without `audit.read` receives 404 `not-found`; a holder of `audit.read` for all entities receives 200 (fragment rev 1.15; 04 API-C-03 rev 1.219); `GET /jobs?kind=RETENTION_SWEEP&state=SUCCEEDED` filters.
      - `::test_r_50_a_cancel_writes_job_cancel_as_the_caller` (header rev 1.37; supervisor rulings R-50 (a), R-62 (b)): a cancellation that changes the job writes `job.cancel` as the caller under the response's request id — for a `RUNNING` job the request, followed by `job.finish` with state `CANCELLED` when the handler stops; for a `QUEUED` job the terminal event itself; a repeated request, a refused one and a request for another member's job write nothing. `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_job_monitoring.py::test_r_50_a_a_job_that_cannot_be_settled_never_stops_the_sweep` (dev-guide DG-KRN-JOB-06 rev 1.89): the sweeper settles each job in isolation — the stalled job of a tenant whose audit chain cannot take `job.finish` stays as it is and the stalled job of the tenant after it ends `FAILED` with its event.
      - `::test_cancel_between_chunks`: `POST /jobs/{id}/cancel` sets `cancel_requested_at`; a chunked test handler observes `cancel_requested()` after its current chunk and the job ends `CANCELLED`.
      - `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_retention.py::test_retention_sweep_deletes_expired_rows`: an `idempotency_record` 7 days + 1 second old, a `user_session` ended 30 days + 1 second earlier and an expired `password_reset_token` are deleted; younger rows remain; the job ends `SUCCEEDED` with `result.counts` naming the three tables.
    - Answer keys: none

- [ ] **PLF-23 Audit read API and chain verification job.**
  - **Prerequisites:** PLF-22
  - **Scope:**

    - API: API-R-10 `GET /audit-events`, `GET /audit-events/verifications`, `POST /audit-events/verify`
    - Engine: none
    - Screens: none
  - **Acceptance:**
    - Tests:
      - `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_audit_verification.py::test_ctl_039_failure_notifies_and_records`: after tampering at chain_seq 2, the `AUDIT_CHAIN_VERIFY` job writes one `audit_chain_verification` row with `result` `FAIL`, `first_failure_seq` 2 and `trigger` `SCHEDULED`; every tenant admin and controller membership that holds its role for all entities — as each of the world's does — receives `CHAIN_VERIFICATION_FAILED` titled `Audit chain verification failed` in-app and by email (a holder for named entities is not told: PLF-14, `test_notification_scope_db.py`).
      - `::test_req_plt_020_pass_writes_digest`: on an intact chain of 5 events, the row has `result` `PASS`, `events_checked` 5 and `digest_last_hmac` equal to the head; `digest_file_id` names a file of purpose `AUDIT_DIGEST` whose JSON holds `tenant_id`, `last_chain_seq` 5, `last_hmac`, `hmac_key_id`, `events_checked` 5 and `verified_at`.
      - `::test_sch_01_fan_out_per_tenant`: the periodic task defers one `AUDIT_CHAIN_VERIFY` job per `ACTIVE` tenant through `platform_session("tenant_directory")` and writes `PLATFORM_SCOPE_USED`.
      - `::test_sch_02_security_chain_verification`: the task records `PASS` for an intact `security_event` chain.
      - `backend/tests/api/test_audit_api.py::test_list_filters`: `GET /audit-events?object_type=role&action=role.create&from=<t0>&to=<t1>` returns matching events newest first; a `viewer` receives 403 `forbidden`.
        - Witnesses of item AUDIT-LIST-PLAN-1 (header rev 1.232, fragment rev 1.33; the supervisor's ruling of 2026-10-02 on the lane's two lines of measurement; 04 rev 1.280; sub-bullets, so the item's criteria count is unchanged):
          - `backend/tests/pg/test_audit_log_plans.py::test_audit_list_plan_1_a_read_plans_the_months_of_its_range`: with the clock on 12 September 2026 the plan of `GET /audit-events` reads the partitions of August and September and no other; with `from` eighty days back and no `to`, June to September; `GET /audit-events/actors` the same two; a read by `chain_seq` takes no end and plans all 181.
          - `backend/tests/unit/test_audit_log_reads.py::test_a_read_ends_at_to_else_one_day_after_the_request` and `::test_the_statement_adds_a_start_and_an_end_only_where_the_caller_sent_none`: the end is `to`, else one day after the request; the resource adds it where the caller sent no `to`, and neither a start nor an end for a read by contract or by sequence.
      - `::test_verify_on_demand_returns_job`: `POST /audit-events/verify` returns 202 with API-S-Job of kind `AUDIT_CHAIN_VERIFY` and `Location: /api/v1/jobs/<id>`; the verification row has `trigger` `ON_DEMAND`; `GET /audit-events/verifications` lists it.
      - Tenant-wide lists and acts under a role held for named entities (fragment rev 1.15; item SCOPE-WORKSPACE-LISTS-1; 04 API-C-03 and API-R-41 rev 1.219; SCREENS_B rev 1.76; WLD-K-04 with roles granted through the product): `backend/tests/api/test_workspace_lists_scope.py::test_the_audit_log_and_the_control_evidence_are_for_holders_of_all_entities` — an Auditor of one entity is answered 403 on the audit events, their actors, `POST /audit-events/verify` and the control executions, each after one `DENIED` event with `scope` `"*"`, and reads the verifications; `::test_a_member_of_one_entity_sees_the_jobs_he_started` — her own job and no other, 404 by id, by subject and to `cancel`; `::test_a_source_record_is_read_through_a_contract_in_reach` — 200 through a contract of her entity, 404 for a record of another entity's contract and for one no contract names; `backend/tests/api/test_tenant_wide_acts_scope.py::test_the_tenant_level_administration_is_for_holders_of_all_entities` — the tenant's settings and currencies, the close checklist templates, the support grants, the snapshots, sandboxes and reset, the webhook endpoints and deliveries: every route refuses the holder of one entity and answers the holder of all; `::test_an_operators_grant_is_decided_by_an_administrator_of_all_entities`; `backend/tests/domain/reports/test_registers_scope_db.py::test_a_register_of_requests_states_the_requests_its_entities_cover` and `::test_a_run_names_no_entity_outside_a_permission_its_report_declares` — RPT-26 and RPT-23 over one entity, the other and both; a Viewer of two entities who audits one
      - Rows that name their own entities, and a migration, under a role held for named entities (fragment rev 1.20; item SCOPE-WORKSPACE-LISTS-1 parts (c2) and (c3); 04 API-C-03, API-R-45 and API-R-48 rev 1.243): `backend/tests/api/test_integration_reach_scope.py::test_a_connection_is_reached_by_a_scope_that_covers_every_entity_it_serves` — an Integration Admin of AVM-US alone is listed and answered the connection of AVM-US with its sync run and external id, and 404 for the connections of AVM-UK, of both entities and of every entity, to a read, a rename, the probe and a sync; an external id is linked under a connection in reach; `::test_a_connection_serves_no_entity_outside_its_administrators_scope` — the re-pointing of AVM-UK's connection answers 404, and an entity outside the caller's scope or the empty list is 422 on `entity_ids` after a `DENIED` event of the command, at creation and at a change; `backend/tests/api/test_migrations_scope.py::test_every_route_of_a_migration_is_for_holders_of_all_entities` — the ten routes of API-R-48.
      - The workspace's reference data under a role held for named entities (fragment rev 1.30; item SCOPE-WORKSPACE-LISTS-1 part (d); 04 API-C-03, API-R-17 and API-R-18 rev 1.276; `worlds.ifrs_sw01` with a second entity AVM-DE): `backend/tests/api/test_reference_settings_scope.py::test_a_new_entity_asks_the_permission_for_all_entities` — a Revenue Accountant of AVM-DE alone, and one who also reads every entity as a Viewer, are refused `POST /entities` 403 by name with a `DENIED` event of `scope` `"*"` before the body is judged; a member of all entities creates the entity and is told a code that is taken; `::test_a_book_of_the_workspace_asks_the_permission_for_all_entities` — `PATCH /books/IFRS15` that would disable the book US01 keeps is refused the member of AVM-DE, the book stays enabled and US01's next computation writes both books; the keeping of the Legacy book for AVM-DE is refused her while the workspace has not enabled it and taken once a member of all entities has; `::test_a_fiscal_year_is_generated_for_every_entity_on_the_calendar` — `POST /calendars` and `generate-year` are refused a member of one entity on the calendar and one of an entity on another; a member of all entities generates FY2028 with `inserted_count` 12 and `inserted_state_count` 36, an invoice of US01 dated 15 Jan 2028 computes, a repeat answers 0 and 0; `::test_the_period_states_of_a_year_are_written_whoever_asks` — `ensure_fiscal_year` in a unit of work whose session reads AVM-DE alone writes the 36 states; `::test_an_entity_is_changed_with_the_permission_for_that_entity` — `PATCH /entities/{US01}` and `PUT /entities/{US01}/books/{code}` are refused 403 by name, with a `DENIED` event, to a member who reads US01 through another role and holds the permission for AVM-DE, 404 to one who does not read it, and taken from a Revenue Accountant of US01.
