    - Engine: none
    - Screens: none
  - **Acceptance:**
    - Tests:
      - `backend/tests/api/test_api_clients.py::test_ctl_037_approval_scope_rejected`: `POST /api-clients {name: "svc-salesforce", scopes: ["contract.read", "contract.approve"]}` returns 422 `scope-not-allowed` with detail `API clients cannot hold approval permissions.`; a direct insert with `scopes {contract.approve}` fails with `EREV-REF-002`.
      - `::test_create_client_secret_shown_once`: a valid create with a fresh step-up returns 201 with `client_id` matching `^erevc_[0-9a-f]{32}_[A-Za-z0-9_-]{22}$`, a `client_secret`, `expires_at` = created + 365 days and `rate_limit_per_minute` 600; `GET /api-clients/{id}` never includes the secret; `secret_hash` starts with `$argon2id$`.
      - `::test_client_credentials_token`: `POST /oauth/token` with `grant_type=client_credentials` and HTTP Basic credentials returns `{access_token, token_type: "Bearer", expires_in: 3600, scope}` with `access_token` matching `^erevt_[0-9a-f]{32}_[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}$`; with scope `audit.read` the token reads `GET /audit-events` with 200, and a client without that scope receives 403 `forbidden`.
      - `::test_token_expiry_revocation_and_rotation`: 61 minutes after issue the token returns 401 `unauthenticated`; after `POST /api-clients/{id}/revoke {reason}` every token of the client returns 401; after `rotate-secret` the old secret cannot obtain a token.
      - `::test_krn_apr_04_api_client_cannot_decide`: a token principal calling `POST /approvals/{id}/approve` receives 403 `forbidden` and no decision is written.
      - `::test_bearer_requests_skip_csrf`: a bearer `POST` without `X-CSRF-Token` passes the CSRF step.
      - BUILD_SPEC header 1.99 (supervisor ruling R-38 (iii); 03 REQ-PLT-033 rev 1.83; 04 T-PLT-15 "Grant and secret" rev 1.168): the tests above are those of a client whose creator is the bootstrap Tenant Admin of a workspace in setup — rule `AUTO-BOOTSTRAP` approves its scopes at submission, so the creation answers `ACTIVE` with the secret, `has_secret` true and `secret_rotated_at` the instant of the creation. For every other creator, `backend/tests/api/test_api_client_grants.py::test_r38_a_client_takes_effect_only_after_another_person_approves`: `POST /api-clients` answers 201 with the client `PENDING_APPROVAL`, no secret and `approval_request_id` — a `ROLE_ASSIGNMENT` request over the client's entities whose preview shows the scopes; no secret can be issued and the client cannot authenticate until another holder of `access.approve` approves; then it is `ACTIVE` with `has_secret` false until `rotate-secret` issues its first secret under step-up, and the approver saw none. `::test_r38_the_requester_cannot_approve_their_own_client`: the requester's own approval is 403 `self-approval`, nothing is decided and the client stays pending; a second Tenant Admin approves it. `::test_r38_a_pending_client_answers_the_token_endpoint_as_an_unknown_client`: a pending client, an approved one before its first secret and a rejected one answer `POST /oauth/token` exactly as an unknown client does. `::test_r38_a_rejected_or_withdrawn_request_leaves_the_client_rejected`: a rejection by the approver and a withdrawal by the requester leave the client `REJECTED`; no secret can be issued for it, and the audit trail names the request that closed it. `::test_r38_the_bootstrap_admin_in_setup_gets_the_client_and_its_secret_at_once`: the request of the bootstrap Tenant Admin alone in setup shows the rule, not a person, and the secret authenticates at once. `::test_apr_setup_rule_2_a_client_requested_when_setup_completion_is_due_waits_for_a_person`: once the conditions of PRD BR-PLT-02 hold the client waits for a person — also where no command has set the stamp — and the creating command sets it (04 T-PLT-01 rev 1.168); `backend/tests/architecture/test_setup_rule_submitters.py` holds that every submitter of a `ROLE_ASSIGNMENT` states the reading (dev-guide DG-KRN-APR-08). `::test_api_client_entity_scope_1_a_client_is_granted_for_entities_its_creator_covers`: `entity_codes` are taken within the creator's own `api_client.manage` scope, the request is bound to those entities, and a client for an entity outside that scope answers as one for an entity that does not exist (item API-CLIENT-ENTITY-SCOPE-1). Rev 1.31 (BUILD_SPEC header 1.227; item API-CLIENT-COMMANDS-SCOPE-1; 04 T-PLT-10 and T-PLT-15 rev 1.278), `::test_api_client_commands_scope_1_a_client_is_read_and_commanded_within_its_entities`: an administrator of one entity is listed neither a client for all entities nor a client of another entity, and reads each as an id that names no client; her `rotate-secret` and `revoke` on each answer what the same command answers for an id that names no client — the secret unchanged and still valid, the client `ACTIVE` — with one `DENIED` event of each attempt under the client, without an entity, and none for the unknown id; her requests beyond her own access are recorded as denials of the creation; she lists and reads her own approved client, issues its first secret and revokes it, and the administrator of all entities lists, reads, rotates and revokes all. The open command's rider (04 API-R-18 rev 1.168), `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_setup_completion.py::test_a_period_is_not_opened_on_settings_manage_once_the_conditions_hold`: `POST /periods/{id}/open` on `settings.manage` is refused once the conditions hold, whether or not the stamp is set; a Controller opens the period and the command sets the stamp.

        - Witnesses of `erev idp domains`, `erev idp disable` and `erev idp enable` (header rev 1.148, fragment rev 1.13; item OPS-IDP-DOMAINS-1, supervisor ruling R-108 (b) (5), and the supervisor's ruling of 2026-10-01 for `erev idp disable` and `erev idp enable`; 03 rev 1.132, 04 rev 1.217, 05 rev 1.156; sub-bullets, so the item's criteria count is unchanged):
          - `::test_ops_idp_domains_1_the_operator_changes_the_domains_of_a_provider`: the command adds and removes domains of provider `mock-oidc` in one transaction with one `PLATFORM_SCOPE_USED` event whose detail names the command `idp.domains`, the provider, the domains added and removed and the identities left outside; no domain named, an unknown provider, a malformed or repeated domain, one domain added and removed, and a result outside 1 to 20 each exit 1 with `validation-failed`, rule `T-PLT-03`, and write nothing; adding a bound domain and removing an unbound one change nothing and do not write the row.
          - `backend/tests/api/test_oidc.py::test_ops_idp_domains_1_a_change_of_domains_holds_from_the_next_sign_in`: an identity of a domain that is added is invited and signed in; after the domain is removed the callback answers 401 `unauthenticated` with `LOGIN_FAILED` reason `email_domain_not_bound`, the identity stays invited and linked, and no session is made.
          - `::test_ops_idp_domains_1_the_operator_takes_a_provider_out_of_sign_in_and_puts_it_back`: `erev idp disable` and `erev idp enable` write `is_enabled` with one `PLATFORM_SCOPE_USED` event each (`idp.disable`, `idp.enable`, the provider and `changed`); an unknown provider exits 1 with rule `T-PLT-03` and writes nothing; a command that changes nothing does not write the row and leaves its event; `erev idp domains` works on a disabled provider.
          - `backend/tests/api/test_oidc.py::test_ops_idp_domains_1_a_disabled_provider_signs_nobody_in`: after `erev idp disable`, `GET /session` lists no provider, the start route answers 404 `not-found`, and the callback of a sign-in that was started before — and one without a flow — answers 401 `unauthenticated` with `LOGIN_FAILED` reason `provider_disabled` while the recording transport receives no request; the identity's link, its open session and its password sign-in are unchanged; after `erev idp enable` the provider is listed and signs the identity in again.
        - Witnesses of `erev idp end-sessions` and of the sign-in's second read of its provider (header rev 1.193, fragment rev 1.23; item OPS-IDP-SESSIONS-1, placed by the supervisor on the lane's report of register index 126, and ruled on the lane's pre-build line on 2026-10-01; 05 rev 1.178, 04 rev 1.249, dev-guide rev 1.238; sub-bullets, so the item's criteria count is unchanged):
          - `backend/tests/api/test_oidc.py::test_ops_idp_sessions_1_a_sign_in_in_flight_reads_the_provider_again`: a callback whose first read found the provider enabled, and whose provider the operator disables while the code is redeemed, answers 401 `unauthenticated` with `LOGIN_FAILED` reason `provider_disabled` on the identity and opens no session.
          - `::test_ops_idp_sessions_1_the_command_ends_what_was_opened_through_the_provider`: after `erev idp disable`, `erev idp end-sessions` ends the open sessions the provider's identities opened through it — each answers 401 at its next request, with `end_reason` `REVOKED` — and leaves the session one of them opened with a password, the session of an identity of another provider and a session that had ended before; it prints the provider, the identities and the sessions ended and writes one `PLATFORM_SCOPE_USED` event with them.
          - `::test_ops_idp_sessions_1_a_sign_in_that_holds_the_identity_is_ended_by_the_command`: a sign-in that has read the provider enabled and holds the identity's row while the operator disables the provider and starts the command — the command is observed waiting for that row, the sign-in commits its session, and the command ends it.
          - `backend/tests/unit/test_cli_idp.py::test_ops_idp_sessions_1_the_operator_ends_the_sessions_of_a_disabled_provider`: an unknown provider and an enabled provider exit 1 with `validation-failed`, rule `T-PLT-03`, and write nothing; on a disabled provider without an open session the command is answered with zero identities and zero sessions and leaves its event.
        - Witnesses of the rotation that outlives no act which ends sessions, and of the lock order of the identity's row (item AUTH-LOCK-ORDER-1) (header rev 1.193, fragment rev 1.23, second rows; item SESSION-ROTATION-END-1, found by the lane in its head of register index 188 before the gates, and ruled into that head by the supervisor on 2026-10-01; 05 rev 1.178 SAR-09 and SAR-10, dev-guide rev 1.238; sub-bullets, so the item's criteria count is unchanged):
          - `backend/tests/api/test_oidc.py::test_ops_idp_sessions_1_a_rotation_past_its_authentication_opens_nothing_after_the_command`: the provider is disabled and its sessions are ended between a request's authentication and its rotation; `POST /session/tenant` answers 401 `unauthenticated`, sets no cookie, and the identity's one session is the one the command ended.
          - `::test_ops_idp_sessions_1_a_rotation_that_holds_the_identity_is_ended_by_the_command`: a rotation that has ended the session it was given and not yet committed its successor — the command is observed waiting for the identity's row, the rotation completes, and the command ends the successor (one identity, one session).
          - `backend/tests/api/test_session.py::test_sar_09_a_rotation_takes_the_identity_then_ends_the_session_it_was_given`: of the statements a rotation sends, one takes the identity's row `FOR NO KEY UPDATE`, and it stands before the end of the given session, which stands before the insert of the successor.
          - `::test_sar_09_a_session_that_ended_after_its_request_was_authenticated_has_no_successor`: a session signed out, and a session rotated by another request, between a request's authentication and its rotation — the rotation answers 401 `unauthenticated` with "Sign in to continue." and opens nothing; of two requests that present one session, one has the successor.
          - `backend/tests/api/test_me_password.py::test_sar_10_a_rotation_in_flight_does_not_outlive_a_password_change`: the password changes between another session's authentication and its rotation; that rotation answers 401, and the only open session is the one that changed the password.
          - `backend/tests/api/test_users_api.py::test_reset_mfa_is_not_outlived_by_a_rotation_past_its_authentication`: an administrator resets a member's factor between her request's authentication and its rotation; her rotation answers 401 and no session of hers is open.
          - `::test_reset_mfa_waits_for_a_rotation_that_holds_the_identity`: her rotation has ended the session it was given and not yet committed its successor — the reset is observed waiting for her identity's row, the rotation completes, and the reset ends the successor.
          - `::test_reset_mfa_takes_the_factor_then_the_identity_then_the_sessions`: of the statements an MFA reset sends, the membership's row is locked before the factor's, the factor's before the identity's, and the identity's before the sessions end.
          - `::test_reset_mfa_and_a_verification_under_way_meet_at_the_factor_and_both_complete`: a verification holds the member's factor, her identity and the session it has ended when her factor is reset — the reset is observed waiting for the factor's row, the verification completes, the reset then disables the factor and ends the rotated session; neither is refused as a deadlock.
          - `backend/tests/api/test_mfa.py::test_sar_09_a_verification_takes_the_factor_then_the_identity_then_the_session`: of the statements a verification sends, the factor's row is locked before the identity's, the identity's before the end of the given session, that before the insert of the successor, and all of them before the security chain's lock; a refused code locks the factor, then the identity, then asks for the chain's lock.
          - `::test_sar_09_a_password_step_beside_the_same_members_code_step_loses_neither`: a password step holds the member's identity row when her code step, with a code that is not hers, begins — the code step is observed waiting for that row (`FOR NO KEY UPDATE`), not for an event's foreign key; the password step answers 200 and the code step then 422. Before, one of the two was answered 409 `lock-conflict`.
          - `backend/tests/api/test_session.py::test_sar_09_a_password_step_holds_the_identity_without_its_key_and_before_the_chain`: a sign-in sends one lock of the identity's row, `FOR NO KEY UPDATE`, before its first request for the security chain's lock; a sign-out of the member's other session is answered 204 while a password step of hers holds the row.
          - `backend/tests/unit/test_identity_row_locks.py::test_dg_krn_auth_08_an_identitys_row_is_locked_without_its_key`: a scan of `erev_api` finds thirteen locks of an `app_user` row in seven modules (rev 1.35: twelve before the acceptance's second look) and none that takes the row's key; the scan itself is held by `::test_dg_krn_auth_08_the_scan_tells_a_keyed_lock_and_another_tables`.
          - `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_support_grants.py::test_sar_09_an_operators_support_session_has_no_successor_once_it_ended`: an operator's session is signed out after the request to open a workspace was authenticated and its grant read — 401 `unauthenticated`, no session under the grant, no event of the grant in the workspace's log.
          - `frontend/src/routes/auth/sign-in.test.tsx` "a session that is live when the page opens leaves for next at once, without the form": the page the client sends a refused tab to reads a live session and returns to `next`; it shows neither the form nor the session-ended notice.
        - Witnesses of item INVITE-ACCEPT-SESSION-1 and of `erev idp list` (header rev 1.234, fragment rev 1.35; the fourth place a session is opened, found by lane OPS's family sweep in its head of register index 188; the supervisor's rulings of 2026-10-02; 05 rev 1.201, dev-guide rev 1.267; sub-bullets, so the item's criteria count is unchanged):
          - `backend/tests/api/test_users_api.py::test_an_acceptance_opens_no_session_on_a_password_changed_after_its_check`: her password changes between the acceptance's verification and its transaction; the acceptance answers 404 `not-found` and sets no cookie, the only open session is the one that changed the password, the membership is still `INVITED`, the old password is then refused 422 and the new one accepts.
          - `::test_an_acceptance_takes_the_membership_then_the_identity_then_the_session`: of the statements an acceptance sends, the membership's update stands before the identity's row (`FOR NO KEY UPDATE`, reading the password's hash) and that before the insert of the session; for a person without a password the membership's update stands before the first password's write.
          - `::test_a_password_change_waits_for_an_acceptance_that_holds_the_identity`: the acceptance has looked a second time and has not yet committed — her password change is observed waiting for her identity's row, the acceptance completes, and the change ends the session opened there; its cookie is answered 401.
          - `::test_the_second_look_answers_for_the_identity_as_it_is_under_its_row`: `sessions.hold_password` sends one lock of the identity's row, `FOR NO KEY UPDATE`; a wrong password, an identity that is `DISABLED`, one without a password and an unknown one are answered no, and no failure is counted.
          - `backend/tests/unit/test_cli_idp.py::test_erev_idp_list_names_the_providers_and_whether_each_is_enabled`: `erev idp list` prints the provider with its code, kind, name, issuer, email domains in order and `is_enabled`, ordered by code among the others; after `erev idp disable` it prints `is_enabled` false; the security chain gains no event and the provider's row no write.

### 04.1 Phase preamble

**Entry criteria.** GATE-PLF is ticked. No other entry criterion (PHASES §1 row WEB).

**Exit criteria (PHASES §1 row WEB).**

1. The frontend source layout of dev-guide §8.1; the format module and i18n (DS-VER-03; DESIGN_SYSTEM §9); DS-CMP-01 to DS-CMP-32 with DS-VER-04 tests; the design gallery X:design (DS-VER-06).
2. The shell of SCREENS §1 with a rail listing built destinations only, and the WEB screens and placements of PHASES §10.
3. The Playwright harness DG-E2E-01 to DG-E2E-12: five projects, `personas` with TOTP, `screens.capture`, `a11y.check`, the network guard and `tenants.ts`; `scripts/e2e.sh` (BS-D-20); `screens.spec.ts` rows for the WEB screens; `design.spec.ts` (DS-VER-06 to DS-VER-09).
4. The `erev seed demo` scaffold: tenants WLD-T-00 to WLD-T-07 with code, display name, kind, `is_demo` and reporting currency (PRD §2.4); persona users WLD-U-01 to WLD-U-11 with memberships and roles (§2.3); TOTP factors (WLD-U-R2); `.run/demo-credentials.txt` (DG-RUN-32); `make seed` and `make e2e`.

**Gate scope at the exit (PHASES §4 row GATE-WEB).** `make ci`; `make test-pg`; `make e2e` unfiltered with BS-D-20 skips; the answer-key loader and coverage unit tests inside `make ci`; `make properties` unfiltered; `erev controls-report --tags-only` inside `make lint`; the light and dark screenshots of every WEB capture read and compared with SCREENS, SCREENS_B and DESIGN_SYSTEM.

**Membership (PHASES §6.4, §10, §13, §15.2).** Requirements REQ-PLT-004, 005, 006, 008, 009, 010, 013, 017, 021, 033, 036, REQ-CTL-006, REQ-SEC-004, REQ-SEC-008, REQ-OPS-016, REQ-UX-001, 002, 006, 007, 008, 009, 012, 013, 016, 021 and 022 (26). Route rows RT-01 to RT-06, RT-54 to RT-58, RT-71 to RT-73, RT-87 to RT-93, RT-97, RT-98, RT-108, RT-109, RT-111 and RT-112 (27). Placements SF-21, SF-23, SF-27, X:session-expiring, X:route-error and X:narrow-viewport. Make targets `seed` and `e2e`. `E2E` hints closing in WEB (§15.2): REQ-PLT-021, REQ-SEC-008, REQ-UX-007, REQ-UX-013, REQ-UX-022. Controls: none. Journeys: none.

**Captures (BS-D-09).** Capture names are the SCR-TID-03 normalisation of the screen id plus an optional state slug. Every screen item appends its rows to `frontend/e2e/projects/screens.spec.ts`, runs `a11y.check` on each surface, and requires the loop to read `frontend/e2e/.screens/screens/<nn>-<capture>.light.png` and `.dark.png` for each capture, compare them with the SCREENS, SCREENS_B and DESIGN_SYSTEM sections cited, and record any finding in `PROGRESS.md` before fixing it (XR-10; GK-07).

  - **Prerequisites:** GATE-PLF
  - **Scope:**
    - Paths:
      - `backend/erev_api/api/v1/session.py` `session_verify_mfa` (D-80 MFA challenge limits):
        - admits the attempt through `app.state.login_rate_limiter`, keyed by client address and the session user's normalised email, before `mfa.verify`. The login and MFA routes share the SAR-13 buckets;
        - a refused attempt answers 429 `rate-limited` with `Retry-After` and writes nothing;
        - `problem_responses` gains `rate-limited` and `account-locked`.
      - `backend/erev_api/auth/mfa.py` `verify` (D-80):
        - a wrong TOTP code or recovery code writes `MFA_CHALLENGE_FAILED`;
        - the fifth such event for the user after the later of `user_mfa_factor.updated_at` and `app_user.locked_until` sets `locked_until` to now + 15 minutes, writes `ACCOUNT_LOCKED` and answers 423 `account-locked`;
        - while `locked_until` is in the future, the route answers 423 `account-locked` without verifying.
      - `backend/erev_api/domain/platform/users.py` (D-80 MFA reset):
        - `reset_mfa` refuses a membership that is neither `ACTIVE` nor `SUSPENDED` with 409 `invalid-transition` (`errors[0].field == "status"`) before any write. A target whose `app_user.is_operator` is true answers 404 `not-found`;
        - `reset_mfa` writes `user_mfa_factor.reset` in the tenant of every ACTIVE membership of the user, as `auth/mfa.py` `_audit_enrolment` does (04 T-PLT-04; SPEC-Q-182 (d) overruled);
        - `invite_user` refuses an email whose `app_user.is_operator` is true with 422 `validation-failed` on `email`, rule `T-PLT-02`, message "This email cannot be invited to a workspace."
      - `backend/erev_api/approvals/subjects.py`:
        - `_apply_role_assignment` locks the membership row `FOR UPDATE` before `sod.assert_assignment_allowed`;
        - `_apply_role_change` locks the membership of every holder of the role, in ascending id order, before `sod.assert_role_change_allowed` (DG-CMD-02; precedent `users._lock_membership`).
      - `backend/erev_api/audit/verify.py` (D-80 chain anchoring):
        - `verify_tenant_chain` compares the last checked event with `audit_chain_head`, read in the same transaction;
        - `record_tenant_verification` compares the latest earlier PASS row's `to_chain_seq` event with its `digest_last_hmac`.
      - `backend/erev_api/db/migrations/versions/0025_audit_chain_head_guard.py`, created with `make revision MSG="audit chain head guard" ITEM=WEB-3a`:
        - it creates `erev.tg_audit_chain_head__guard()` and trigger `tg_audit_chain_head__guard` `BEFORE UPDATE ON erev.audit_chain_head`;
        - the trigger refuses with `EREV-AUD-001` any update issued outside `tg_audit_event__chain` (`pg_trigger_depth() < 2`) or not setting `last_chain_seq = OLD.last_chain_seq + 1`;
        - `downgrade()` drops both.
      - Tests: `backend/tests/api/test_mfa.py`, `backend/tests/api/test_users_api.py`, `backend/tests/api/test_roles_api.py`, `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_approval_engine.py`, `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_audit_verification.py`, `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_audit_writer.py`, `backend/tests/pg/test_chains.py`, `backend/tests/pg/test_db_invariants.py`.
      - `docs/api/openapi.json` and `frontend/src/lib/api/schema.d.ts` through `make openapi`.

  - **Acceptance:**
    - Tests:
      - `backend/tests/api/test_mfa.py::test_sar_13_mfa_rate_limit` (PR-A-01):
        - Setup: user U with a confirmed factor signs in with the password only (`mfa_verified_at` null) in an earlier FrozenClock minute.
        - Within one minute from one client address, wrong codes to `POST /api/v1/session/mfa` give:
          - attempts 1 to 4: 422 `validation-failed`;
          - attempt 5: 423 `account-locked`;
          - attempts 6 to 10: 423 without verification;
          - attempt 11: 429 `rate-limited` with a `Retry-After` header.
        - `MFA_CHALLENGE_FAILED` is written 5 times and `ACCOUNT_LOCKED` once.
        - Ten other users, signed in during an earlier minute, each send 5 wrong codes from one address within one minute (50 requests). The 51st request from that address, for an eleventh user, returns 429 `rate-limited` and writes no `MFA_CHALLENGE_FAILED`.
        - After the clock advances 16 minutes, U's correct code returns 200 with `mfa_verified_at` set.
        - The review probe `test_probe_mfa_challenge_unthrottled` observed 150 × 422 in this situation.
      - `backend/tests/api/test_mfa.py::test_ctl_033_mfa_lockout_after_five_failures` (PR-A-01):
        - U sends 4 wrong codes (422), signs in with the password again, then sends 1 wrong code: 423 `account-locked`. `app_user.locked_until` equals the FrozenClock time + 15 minutes.
        - While U is locked, a correct code returns 423, and the session's `mfa_verified_at` stays null.
        - For a second user, 4 wrong TOTP codes and 1 wrong recovery code lock the account.
        - 15 minutes later U's correct code returns 200, and a following wrong code returns 422.
        - `test_ctl_033_mfa_required_permission_blocks_until_verified`, `test_recovery_code_single_use` and `test_bs1_d_19_step_up_window` still pass.
      - `backend/tests/api/test_users_api.py::test_reset_mfa_refused_for_unaccepted_invitation` (PR-Z-01):
        - Setup: the admin of workspace B is `ACTIVE` in B, with a confirmed factor and an open session.
        - The Tenant Admin of workspace A, with a fresh step-up, sends `POST /users {email: <B admin email>, display_name: "Name typed by A", roles: [{role_id: <viewer>, is_all_entities: true, entity_codes: []}]}`: 201 `INVITED`.
        - `POST /users/{that membership}/reset-mfa {reason: "probe cross tenant reset"}` returns 409 `invalid-transition` with `errors[0].field == "status"`, and then:
          - the factor's `disabled_at` stays null;
          - the B admin's session still gets 200 on `GET /api/v1/users`;
          - no `MFA_RESET` security event and no `user_mfa_factor.reset` audit event is written.
        - After the B admin accepts the invitation (membership `ACTIVE`), the command with a new `Idempotency-Key` returns 200.
        - The review probe P-3 observed 200 and the victim's sessions ended.
        - `test_reset_mfa_step_up_and_sessions` still passes.
      - `backend/tests/api/test_users_api.py::test_operator_identity_not_invitable_or_resettable` (PR-Z-01):
        - Setup: an operator identity created through `auth.operators.create_operator`, enrolled, with one open session.
        - `POST /users` with that email from workspace A returns 422 `validation-failed` with `errors[0] = {field: "email", rule_id: "T-PLT-02"}`, and no membership is written.
        - A membership of A for that identity, inserted as `erev_owner` with status `ACTIVE`, makes `POST /users/{id}/reset-mfa` return 404 `not-found`. The operator's factor and session are unchanged.
      - `backend/tests/api/test_users_api.py::test_reset_mfa_audited_in_every_active_workspace` (PR-C-04):
        - Setup: Lena is `ACTIVE` in workspaces A and B and `INVITED` in C.
        - A reset from A returns 200.
        - Exactly one `user_mfa_factor.reset` audit event exists in A and one in B, and none in C.
        - One `MFA_RESET` security event is written, and every session of Lena ends `REVOKED`.
        - The review observed A=1, B=0.
      - `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_approval_engine.py::test_ctl_034_concurrent_conflicting_approvals_serialised` (PR-K-02):
        - Setup: setup completed. Member M holds no role and has two `PENDING` `ROLE_ASSIGNMENT` requests, for `revenue_accountant` and `revenue_reviewer`.
        - Approver A's `decide` of the first runs on its own connection and holds its transaction open after `_apply_role_assignment`.
        - Approver B's `decide` of the second runs on a second connection under `SET LOCAL lock_timeout = '500ms'`. It raises lock-not-available (SQLSTATE `55P03`) and commits nothing.
        - After A commits, B's `decide` in a new transaction raises 409 `sod-conflict` naming `SoD-3` and rolls back. Then:
          - B's request stays `PENDING`;
          - M holds exactly one active assignment;
          - `sod.conflict_report(as_of=now)` lists no uncovered conflict for M.
        - A `ROLE_CHANGE` approval for a role held by a member whose membership another open transaction has locked raises `55P03` under the same `lock_timeout`.
        - The review probe observed both roles granted in 8 of 8 trials.
      - `backend/tests/api/test_roles_api.py::test_ctl_034_second_pending_grant_refused_at_approval` (PR-C-03):
        - Setup: setup completed.
        - Tomas sends `POST /role-assignments {membership_id: <Lena>, role_id: <revenue_accountant>, is_all_entities: true}`, then the same for `revenue_reviewer`: both return 201 `REQUESTED`.
        - Grace approves the first (200) and then the second: 409 `sod-conflict` naming `SoD-3`.
        - The request statuses are `[APPROVED, PENDING]`, and Lena holds one active assignment.
        - The test fails when `_apply_role_assignment` swallows the SoD problem (review probe G3e).
      - `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_audit_verification.py::test_ctl_039_truncated_chain_fails` (PR-K-01):
        - **(a) Tail truncation.** A bare tenant of 5 events (`tests/support/rows.insert_audited_tenant`). As `erev_owner` with `app.data_fix_ticket` set, delete `chain_seq` 4 and 5; `audit_chain_head.last_chain_seq` stays 5. `record_tenant_verification(trigger="ON_DEMAND")` writes a row with:
          - `result` `FAIL`, `to_chain_seq` 3 and `first_failure_seq` 4;
          - `failure_detail.reason` "the chain ends before the head";
          - `digest_file_id` null.
          It also notifies `CHAIN_VERIFICATION_FAILED`.
        - **(b) Truncation with a rewound head.**
          - A second tenant of 5 events is verified `PASS` (`to_chain_seq` 5; the verification's own event is `chain_seq` 6).
          - As `erev_owner` with a ticket: delete `chain_seq` 5 and 6, disable `tg_audit_chain_head__guard`, set the head to `chain_seq` 4 with its hmac, then re-enable the guard.
          - The next verification writes `FAIL` with `first_failure_seq` 5 and reason "a verified event is missing or changed".
        - The review probes observed PASS in both cases.
        - `test_ctl_039_failure_notifies_and_records` and `test_req_plt_020_pass_writes_digest` still pass.
      - `backend/tests/pg/test_db_invariants.py::test_db_09_chain_head_advances_only_through_append` (PR-K-01):
        - As `erev_app` in the tenant context, `UPDATE erev.audit_chain_head SET last_chain_seq = last_chain_seq - 1` fails with `EREV-AUD-001`.
        - An update that writes the current values fails the same way.
        - One `uow.audit(...)` followed by commit still advances the head by one.
        - `test_db_09_audit_chain_continuity` still passes.
      - `backend/tests/pg/test_chains.py::test_ctl_038_actor_tamper_detected` (PR-C-02): as `erev_owner` with a data-fix ticket, set `actor_id` of `chain_seq` 2 to a new uuid. `verify_tenant_chain` returns `FAIL` with `first_failure_seq` 2.
      - `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_audit_writer.py::test_krn_aud_03_hmac_covers_every_column` (PR-C-02):
        - For every column of `audit_event.c` except `hmac`, a row copy with that column changed to a different value of its type gives a different `canonical_event(row)`. Changing `hmac` leaves it unchanged.
        - The test fails under review probe G2b (canonical form without `actor_id`, `actor_kind`, `outcome`, `action` and `object_id`).

- [ ] **WEB-3b PLF review remediation, part 2: one-time secrets, password-change rotation, outbound destination guard and invitation identity data (PR-A-02, PR-A-03, PR-A-04, PR-R-08, PR-Z-02). Supervisor item.**
  - **Prerequisites:** GATE-PLF; WEB-3a
  - **Scope:**
    - Paths:
      - One-time secrets (D-80; SPEC-Q-189 (b) and SPEC-Q-190 (b) overruled):
        - `backend/erev_api/api/deps.py` `run_command` and `backend/erev_api/idempotency/store.py` `complete`: a command may give the body to store separately from the body it returns;
        - `backend/erev_api/api/v1/api_clients.py` (create, rotate-secret) and `backend/erev_api/api/v1/webhooks.py` (create) store the body with `client_secret` or `signing_secret` null;
        - the response schemas declare that member nullable.
      - Password change (D-80):
        - `backend/erev_api/auth/credentials.py` `change_password` calls `sessions.reissue` in the same transaction, and the presented session ends `REVOKED`;
        - `backend/erev_api/api/v1/me.py` `POST /me/password` answers 204 with the new `erev_session` cookie.
      - Outbound destinations (D-80; SPEC-Q-192 (f) overruled):
        - `backend/erev_api/adapters/http/guard.py` `check_destination` applies the address rule;
        - `backend/erev_api/adapters/idp/oidc_http.py` passes every discovery, JWKS and token URL through `check_destination(url, env=settings.env)` and connects to the checked address with SNI, as `adapters/http/webhook_client.py` does;
        - `backend/erev_api/auth/oidc.py` `_issuer_valid(url, *, env)`;
        - `backend/erev_api/cli.py` `erev idp create` passes the environment;
        - `backend/erev_api/adapters/email/smtp.py` checks `EREV_SMTP_HOST` before connecting.
      - Invitation identity data (D-80; SPEC-Q-182 (c) overruled in part):
        - `backend/erev_api/domain/platform/users.py` `member_select`, `user_outs` and `update_user`;
        - `backend/erev_api/schemas/users.py`: `mfa_enrolled` becomes nullable.

  - **Acceptance:**
    - Tests:
      - `backend/tests/api/test_api_clients.py::test_client_secret_not_kept_for_replay` (PR-A-02):
        - A Tenant Admin with a fresh step-up sends `POST /api/v1/api-clients {name: "svc-probe", scopes: ["audit.read"]}` with `Idempotency-Key: k-sup-plf-2-api-client-0001`: 201 with a 43-character `client_secret`.
        - The `idempotency_record` of that key is `COMPLETED`. Its `response_body` holds `client_secret` null and does not contain the returned secret as a substring, and `api_client.secret_hash` starts with `$argon2id$`.
        - Replaying the key returns 201 with `Idempotent-Replay: true` and `client_secret` null.
        - `POST /api/v1/api-clients/{id}/rotate-secret` with a new key behaves the same, and the rotated secret authenticates `POST /oauth/token`.
        - The review probe observed the plaintext stored for 7 days and returned on replay.
        - `test_create_client_secret_shown_once` and `test_token_expiry_revocation_and_rotation` still pass.
      - `backend/tests/api/test_webhooks_api.py::test_signing_secret_not_kept_for_replay` (PR-A-02):
        - `POST /api/v1/webhook-endpoints` with `webhook.manage` and a key returns 201 with a 43-character `signing_secret`.
        - The stored `response_body` holds `signing_secret` null and not the secret, and a replay returns 201 with `signing_secret` null.
        - Deliveries to the endpoint are still signed with the issued secret.
        - `test_create_endpoint_shows_secret_once` and `test_krn_evt_07_signature_header` still pass.
      - `backend/tests/api/test_me_password.py::test_change_password` (PR-A-03; amended per D-80):
        - A wrong current password returns 422 `validation-failed` with `errors[0].field == "current_password"`.
        - Success returns 204 with a `Set-Cookie` `erev_session` whose value differs from the presented one. Then:
          - the presented cookie returns 401 `unauthenticated` on `GET /api/v1/me`, and its `user_session.end_reason` is `REVOKED`;
          - the new cookie returns 200 on `GET /api/v1/me`;
          - the user's other sessions end `PASSWORD_CHANGED`, and `PASSWORD_CHANGED` is written once.
        - This replaces the assertion at `test_me_password.py:82-84` that the presented token keeps working.
      - `backend/tests/unit/test_oidc_client.py::test_sar_15_provider_urls_guarded` (PR-A-04):
        - Setup: `HttpxOidcHttp` built with `env="production"`, a fake resolver (`idp.example` → 93.184.216.34, `internal.example` → 10.0.0.5, `meta.example` → 169.254.169.254) and a recording `httpx.MockTransport`.
        - Fetching `https://idp.example/.well-known/openid-configuration` sends one request to 93.184.216.34 with SNI `idp.example`.
        - `https://internal.example/token` and `https://meta.example/jwks` raise `DestinationRefused`, and the transport records no request.
        - `http://idp.example/x` is refused for its scheme.
        - With `env="test"`, `http://127.0.0.1:8190/api/v1/__mocks__/oidc/.well-known/openid-configuration` is allowed.
      - `backend/tests/api/test_oidc.py::test_sar_15_callback_refuses_private_endpoints` (PR-A-04):
        - The mock IdP's discovery document names `token_endpoint` `http://10.0.0.5/internal-admin` and `jwks_uri` `http://169.254.169.254/latest/meta-data/`.
        - The callback returns 401 `unauthenticated`, `LOGIN_FAILED` carries `detail.reason` `provider_request_failed`, and the recording transport receives no request for either URL.
        - The review probe observed a POST of the token form to `10.0.0.5`.
        - `test_links_existing_user_by_verified_email` still passes.
      - `backend/tests/unit/test_cli_idp.py::test_idp_create_refuses_loopback_http_issuer_in_production` (PR-A-04):
        - With injected settings whose `env` is `production`, `erev idp create --issuer-url http://127.0.0.1:9000/realms/x` (other arguments as in the existing test) exits 1, names rule `T-PLT-03` and writes no `identity_provider` row.
        - `_issuer_valid("http://127.0.0.1:9000/realms/x", env="production")` is false, and with `env="test"` it is true.
        - `test_idp_create_writes_platform_security_event` still passes.
      - `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_webhooks.py::test_sar_15_destination_guard` (PR-R-08; extended):
        - Under `env=production`, `check_destination` refuses `64:ff9b::a9fe:a9fe`, `64:ff9b::7f00:1`, `::a9fe:a9fe`, `::7f00:1`, `2002:a9fe:a9fe::1`, `2002:0a00:0005::1`, `fec0::1`, `ff02::1`, `224.0.0.1`, `255.255.255.255`, `240.0.0.1`, `198.18.0.1` and `192.0.0.170`.
        - It still refuses every SAR-15 range and allows `93.184.216.34`.
      - `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_outbox.py::test_sar_15_smtp_host_guarded` (PR-R-08):
        - Under `env=production`, the SMTP adapter with `EREV_SMTP_HOST` resolving to 10.0.0.5 raises `DestinationRefused` before any socket connects (the socket guard fixture records no connect).
        - The fake email path under `test` is unchanged, and `test_ntr_05_fake_email_file` still passes.
      - `backend/tests/api/test_users_api.py::test_invitation_of_existing_user_discloses_nothing` (PR-Z-02):
        - Setup: the admin of workspace B is `ACTIVE` in B, with display name "b admin real name", `last_login_at` set and a confirmed factor.
        - The Tenant Admin of A sends `POST /users {email: <B admin email>, display_name: "Name typed by A", roles: [viewer]}`: 201 with `status` `INVITED`, `display_name` equal to the email, `last_login_at` null and `mfa_enrolled` null. `GET /users/{id}` and the `GET /users` row show the same.
        - After the B admin accepts the invitation, `GET /users/{id}` shows "b admin real name", the real `last_login_at` and `mfa_enrolled` true.
        - The review probe P-2 observed the real name, last sign-in and MFA status disclosed.
        - `test_invite_during_setup_auto_approved` still passes.
      - `backend/tests/api/test_users_api.py::test_rename_only_users_created_by_the_invitation` (PR-Z-02):
        - Setup: workspace C is provisioned with admin `c-admin@<code>.test`, who has never signed in.
        - The Tenant Admin of A invites that email (201). `PATCH /users/{id} {display_name: "Renamed from tenant A"}` with the current `If-Match` then returns 409 `invalid-transition` with `errors[0].field == "status"`, and `app_user.display_name` stays `c-admin@<code>.test`.
        - Renaming a user that A's own invitation created still returns 200.
        - The review probe P-5 observed 200 and the global name changed.
        - `test_list_detail_update_reactivate_remove` still passes.

- [ ] **WEB-3c PLF review remediation, part 3: single-claim outbox and webhook dispatch, handler heartbeats and stranded-task recovery (PR-R-01, PR-R-02, PR-R-03, PR-R-04). Supervisor item.**
  - **Prerequisites:** GATE-PLF; WEB-3b
  - **Scope:**
    - Paths:
      - `backend/erev_api/events/webhooks.py` `_claim`, `_attempt`, `_record` and `deliver` (D-80 dispatch and job recovery; SPEC-Q-189 (c) corrected):
        - one due delivery is claimed (`FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`, with `next_attempt_at` = now + 120 s as the claim stamp) and committed, then posted, then recorded only where `next_attempt_at` still equals the stamp;
        - zero recorded rows means another deliverer settled the delivery, and the run continues without raising;
        - `jc.heartbeat()` runs after each delivery, with at most 100 deliveries per run.
      - `backend/erev_api/events/outbox.py` `_claim`, `_dispatch`, `_relay` and `relay`:
        - the same single-claim loop over messages, with `updated_at` as the claim stamp;
        - the ADP-32 re-claim takes only messages whose own stamp is older than 15 minutes;
        - `jc.heartbeat()` runs after each message.
      - `backend/erev_api/domain/platform/audit_jobs.py`: `jc.heartbeat()` at least every 1,000 verified events or 30 seconds.
      - `backend/erev_api/jobs/registry.py` `run_job` and `_move`:
        - completion is recorded only on the RUNNING row of the attempt that ran (same `procrastinate_job_id` and attempt number);
        - `_defer_unless_pending` counts only QUEUED rows whose Procrastinate task is `todo` or `doing`.
      - `backend/erev_api/jobs/sweeper.py` (05 JOB-06 part one and SCH-04; SPEC-Q-187 (b) overruled):
        - re-dispatches QUEUED job rows whose Procrastinate task is `failed`, `cancelled` or `aborted`, within the kind's retry profile;
        - retries tasks stalled in `doing` found through `JobManager.get_stalled_jobs` (worker heartbeats), using `retry_job_by_id` (Procrastinate 3.9.0);
        - past the last attempt, the row ends `FAILED` with problem `job-stalled`.
      - `backend/erev_api/worker.py`: only if the periodic sweeper wiring changes.
      - Tests: `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_webhooks.py`, `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_outbox.py`, `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_job_monitoring.py`, `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_jobs.py`.
      - The runbook sections "Stuck and failed jobs" and "Outbound webhooks" (SPEC-Q-187 a; SPEC-Q-189 a).
    - Schema: none
    - API: none
    - Engine: none
    - Screens: none
  - **Acceptance:**
    - Tests:
      - `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_webhooks.py::test_ntr_12_one_post_per_attempt_when_the_lease_lapses` (PR-R-01):
        - Setup: three `period.locked` events are emitted to one active endpoint.
        - An SCH-12 `WEBHOOK_DELIVERY` job posts the first delivery. The recording transport then advances the FrozenClock 121 s and runs a second `deliver()` to completion before the first job continues.
        - Every envelope id is posted exactly once, every delivery is `DELIVERED` after one attempt, and both jobs end `SUCCEEDED`.
        - The review probe A observed `[1, 2, 2]` POSTs and two `invalid-transition` failures.
        - `test_retry_backoff_and_abandon` and `test_inactive_endpoint_abandons_delivery` still pass.
      - `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_outbox.py::test_adp_32_live_relay_not_reclaimed` (PR-R-02):
        - Setup: three `EMAIL` messages are enqueued.
        - Relay 1 dispatches the first message. The fake email adapter then advances the clock 15 minutes and runs relay 2 to completion before relay 1 continues.
        - The fake email directory holds exactly three files, every message is `DISPATCHED`, and both relays end `SUCCEEDED`.
        - A message left `DISPATCHING` by a relay that stopped is still re-claimed and sent once after 15 minutes.
        - The review probe B observed `[1, 2, 2]` sends.
        - `test_krn_evt_05_relay_backoff_and_dead_letter` still passes.
      - `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_job_monitoring.py::test_krn_job_04_heartbeat_keeps_long_handlers_running` (PR-R-03):
        - A `WEBHOOK_DELIVERY` run over 70 due deliveries uses a recording transport that advances the FrozenClock 10 s per POST and calls `sweeper.fail_stalled(runtime)` after every sixth POST. The run stays `RUNNING` throughout, ends `SUCCEEDED`, posts 70 times and is never re-queued.
        - A user's `OUTBOX_RELAY` over 70 messages with the same clock pattern ends `SUCCEEDED`, and no `JOB_FAILED` notification is written.
        - `test_ctl_040_stalled_job_failed_and_notified` still passes.
      - `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_job_monitoring.py::test_run_job_records_only_its_own_attempt` (PR-R-03):
        - Setup: attempt 1 of a `WEBHOOK_DELIVERY` job is `RUNNING`. After 10 minutes 1 second without a heartbeat, the sweeper re-queues it, and attempt 2 moves the row to `RUNNING`.
        - When attempt 1's handler returns, the row stays `RUNNING` under attempt 2 and no result is stored.
        - Attempt 2's completion sets `SUCCEEDED`.
        - The review probe V5 observed attempt 1 marking attempt 2's row `SUCCEEDED`.
      - `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_jobs.py::test_krn_job_06_stranded_tasks_redispatched` (PR-R-04):
        - Setup: an `OUTBOX_RELAY` job is dispatched, and `registry.job_concurrency` is patched to raise `OperationalError`. `run_job` raises, and the Procrastinate task ends `failed` while the row stays `QUEUED`.
        - `outbox.sweep` still defers a relay for the tenant while due messages exist.
        - `sweeper.sweep` re-dispatches the job with a new `procrastinate_job_id`, and the job then ends `SUCCEEDED`.
        - A `WEBHOOK_DELIVERY` task stalled in `doing`, whose worker stopped sending heartbeats (`get_stalled_jobs` with `seconds_since_heartbeat` 30), is retried the same way.
        - `defer_for_active_tenants` again defers `AUDIT_CHAIN_VERIFY` and `RETENTION_SWEEP` for the tenant.
        - Review probes C and V6 observed the job still `QUEUED` and 0 deferrals two days later.
        - `test_krn_job_06_sweeper_requeues_undispatched_jobs` still passes.
    - Answer keys: none

  - **Prerequisites:** GATE-PLF; WEB-3c
  - **Scope:**
    - Paths:
      - Configuration insert guard (D-80; SPEC-Q-151 overruled): `backend/erev_api/db/migration_ops.py` `CONFIG_VERSION_BODY` and `backend/erev_api/db/migrations/versions/0026_config_version_insert_guard.py`, created with `make revision MSG="config version insert guard" ITEM=WEB-3d`:
        - `CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION erev.tg_config_version()` refuses an INSERT with `status <> 'DRAFT'` unless `current_setting('app.platform_scope', true) = 'provisioning'` (`EREV-CFG-002`);
        - `downgrade()` restores the previous body.
      - SoD rules as of a date (D-80; SPEC-Q-150 overruled):
        - `backend/erev_api/auth/sod.py` `_published_rules` and `conflict_report`;
        - `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_sod.py` replaces the assertion at `:240-247`.
      - Upload limits on inflated bytes: `backend/erev_api/files/policy.py` `zip_within_limits` and `_ooxml_media_type`:
        - entries are read through bounded streaming reads that count inflated bytes;
        - the UPL-04 entry, total and ratio limits apply to the actual output;
        - an entry whose output exceeds its declared `file_size`, or whose local-header and central-directory sizes differ, is refused.
      - Streamed body limits (D-80 upload bodies): `backend/erev_api/api/middleware.py` `_receive_within_limit` and `backend/erev_api/api/v1/files.py`.
      - Launcher ports (D-80): `Makefile` and `scripts/proc.sh`:
        - `EREV_API_PORT`, `EREV_WEB_PORT`, `EREV_E2E_API_PORT` and `EREV_E2E_WEB_PORT` come from the environment, else from the `.env` named by `EREV_DOTENV` (default `.env`), else the defaults;
        - `WEB_ENV` passes `EREV_WEB_PORT` to the web process;
        - no value is ever printed.
      - `backend/erev_api/db/types.py`: `MoneyType`, `ExactType` and `FxRateType` set `cache_ok = True`.
      - Tests: `backend/tests/pg/test_db_invariants.py`, `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_sod.py`, `backend/tests/unit/test_upload_policy.py`, `backend/tests/api/test_middleware.py`, `backend/tests/unit/test_makefile_targets.py`, `backend/tests/unit/test_proc_sh.py`, `backend/tests/unit/test_db_types.py`.
      - `docs/guides/` developer notes on review-worktree ports (XR-16).
    - Schema: DB-04 `erev.tg_config_version()` INSERT rule (D-80). No table, column or enum change
    - API: `POST /api/v1/files` (PLF-8) without `Content-Length` returns 422 `validation-failed` with `errors[0].rule_id == "API-C-17"`
    - Engine: none
    - Screens: none
  - **Acceptance:**
    - Tests:
      - `backend/tests/pg/test_db_invariants.py::test_db_04_insert_outside_draft_refused` (PR-K-03):
        - As `erev_app` in a provisioned tenant's context, with no platform scope, each of these inserts fails with `EREV-CFG-002`: a `sod_rule` version with `status` `PUBLISHED`; a `sod_rule` version with `status` `APPROVED`; a `registry_version` with `status` `PUBLISHED`. Each carries `approval_request_id` NULL and a valid `content_sha256`.
        - In one transaction, superseding SoD-3 v1 and then inserting v2 `PUBLISHED` fails, and SoD-3 still conflicts for `revenue_accountant` with `revenue_reviewer`.
        - The same inserts inside `platform_session("provisioning", …)` succeed.
        - The review probe `db04.log` observed the conflict removed with 0 approval requests.
        - `test_db_04_published_sod_rule_frozen`, `test_db_04_published_overlap_rejected` and `test_krn_ten_01_every_plf_seed_row` still pass.
      - `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_sod.py::test_conflict_report_as_of_uses_rule_in_force_then` (PR-C-05):
        - Setup: provisioning at FrozenClock t0 publishes SoD-3 v1. A membership holds `revenue_accountant` and `revenue_reviewer` from t0, as rows written directly (a legacy conflict).
        - `conflict_report(as_of=t0 + 1 day)` lists SoD-3.
        - At t1 = t0 + 10 days, a `ROLE_CHANGE` approval publishes v2 (`ssp.create` × `ssp.approve`) and supersedes v1. Afterwards:
          - `conflict_report(as_of=t0 + 1 day)` still lists SoD-3 with the v1 function sets;
          - `conflict_report(as_of=t1 + 1 second)` does not list it.
        - An assignment revoked at t2 counts for `as_of < t2` and not for `as_of >= t2`.
        - `test_krn_perm_03_rules_from_published_rows_only` asserts that a SUPERSEDED version counts for instants in `[published_at, effective_to)`, and that DRAFT, TESTED and SUBMITTED versions count at no date.
        - `test_conflict_report_as_of` still passes.
      - `backend/tests/unit/test_upload_policy.py::test_upl_04_declared_sizes_not_trusted` (PR-R-05):
        - Setup: an xlsx built in the test whose `[Content_Types].xml` inflates to 100 MiB, with `file_size` patched to 1,500 in both the local header and the central directory, and the CRC of the first 1,500 bytes (review probe `zip_declared_size_bomb.py`).
        - `check_upload(purpose=IMPORT_SOURCE)` raises 422 `upload-type-not-allowed`.
        - A counting wrapper shows that no entry read inflated more than 1,500 bytes + 64 KiB.
        - An archive whose local-header and central-directory sizes differ is refused.
        - `test_upl_04_zip_limits` still passes.
      - `backend/tests/api/test_middleware.py::test_api_c_17_chunked_upload_needs_declared_length` (PR-R-06):
        - Setup: a chunked `POST /api/v1/files` without `Content-Length` and without a session, whose body generator yields 64 KiB chunks up to 256 MiB.
        - The request returns 422 `validation-failed` with `errors[0].rule_id == "API-C-17"`, and the generator yielded at most one chunk.
        - The review verifier observed 192 MiB consumed before the 401.
        - `test_upload_limits_per_purpose` still passes.
      - `backend/tests/api/test_middleware.py::test_api_c_17_body_limit_without_declared_length` (PR-R-06; amended):
        - A chunked 4 MiB body to a 1 MiB route returns 422 `validation-failed` with `rule_id` `API-C-17`, after the generator yielded at most 1 MiB plus one 64 KiB chunk.
        - A probe app receives the first chunk before the body ends (no pre-buffering).
      - `backend/tests/unit/test_makefile_targets.py::test_dg_run_01_port_override` (PR-R-07, S-2; extended):
        - Setup: `EREV_API_PORT` and `EREV_WEB_PORT` are absent from the environment, and `EREV_DOTENV` names a scratch file under `.run/tmp/` holding `EREV_API_PORT=8192`, `EREV_WEB_PORT=5272` and a sentinel line `EREV_SENTINEL=do-not-print`.
        - `make -n dev-up` shows `proc.sh start api 8192` and `proc.sh start web 5272`, the web command line carries `EREV_WEB_PORT=5272`, and stdout does not contain `do-not-print`.
        - An exported `EREV_API_PORT=8193` wins over the file.
        - `make -n dev-up API_PORT=8192 WEB_PORT=5272` passes `EREV_WEB_PORT=5272` to the web process.
        - The existing `backend` and `frontend` assertions still pass.
      - `backend/tests/unit/test_proc_sh.py::test_dg_run_10_ports_from_dotenv` (PR-R-07): with no exported port and `EREV_DOTENV` naming a scratch file that holds `EREV_WEB_PORT=5272`, `scripts/proc.sh status` reports `port=5272` for `web`. `test_dg_run_10_readiness_uses_start_port` still passes.
      - `backend/tests/unit/test_db_types.py::test_krn_money_05_types_cacheable` (S-4):
        - `MoneyType()`, `ExactType()` and `FxRateType()` each have a `_static_cache_key` that is not `sqlalchemy.sql.cache_key.NO_CACHE`.
        - Compiling a `select` over a money column under `warnings.simplefilter("error", SAWarning)` raises nothing.

### 23.1 Phase preamble

**Exit criteria (PHASES §1 row SOP).** T-PLT-39 and API-R-52 (control evidence registry); engine release stamping and `make release-manifest` (05 REL-01 to REL-06); `REPLAY_VERIFY` upgrade validation; rate limits; `GET /metrics`; the privacy commands `user.anonymise` and `file.shred` (05 PRV-07); `erev doctor` complete (05 SAR-40, REL-07); an audit coverage test over every action of REQ-PLT-019; the SAR-42 security tests; the security documents and `itgc-guide.md` (BS-D-13); every `PENDING_` tuple empty (BS-D-07); `make controls-report` passes 49 of 49.

    - Tests:
      - `backend/tests/domain/contracts/test_replay_verify.py::test_rcp_28_same_version_replay_matches`: replaying a stored computation of a contract built through the CTR factories under the same `ENGINE_VERSION` returns `matches` true and writes a `computation_verification` row with trigger `REPLAY_VERIFY`, `outcome = MATCH`, `input_file_sha256_observed = input_file_sha256_expected` and `actual_output_sha256` equal to the source's T-CON-08 `output_sha256`; no posting; `contract_computation`, `contract_version`, `subledger_posting` row counts and `combination_group.head_computation_id` / `dirty_since` are unchanged (D-96, rev 1.2).
      - `::test_rcp_28_mismatch_raises_integrity_incident`: with the stored T-CON-08 `output_sha256` altered by the owner under a data-fix ticket, the verification row is `MISMATCH` reason `EXPECTED_HASH_DISAGREEMENT`, the job ends `SUCCEEDED_WITH_EXCEPTIONS` with exception item `ENGINE`, records the mismatch with the group id, writes the log event `replay_verify.mismatch` with `severity` `SEV-1`, and the stored rows are untouched (D-96, rev 1.2).
      - `::test_rcp_29_sampling`: a tenant with 3 groups with open-period activity and 400 other groups defers `REPLAY_VERIFY` for the 3 groups plus 20 sampled groups (5% of 400 is 20); with 100 other groups it still samples 20; fewer than 20 remaining takes them all; the sample is stratified over every `{book, method}` pair of all enabled books so that each pair present keeps at least one group (a multi-stratum group counted once; coverage above nominal recorded); a group with open activity and no head is reported as `NO_SOURCE` and never blocks; the selection is deterministic for seed 20260912 and identical after a forced restart (D-96, rev 1.2).
      - `::test_rel_06_minor_upgrade_difference_fails_release`: for a simulated MINOR version change, an L1 `DIFFERENCES` outcome makes the validation `FAILED` (terminal for the tenant and release), the frozen `upgrade_validation` report lists the group and difference, no posting occurs and no `approval_request` exists — no approval is offered for MINOR (D-96, rev 1.2; the former "until the difference is approved" is struck).
      - `::test_rel_06_major_upgrade_difference_needs_approval`: for a simulated MAJOR change, L1 `DIFFERENCES` → `PENDING_APPROVAL` with an `ENGINE_RELEASE_VALIDATION` request binding `dataset_sha256`; approval → `APPROVED`, `enabled_at` set, only the `DIFFERENCES` groups dirtied (an earlier `dirty_since` preserved), one `PENDING` T-PLT-46 row per group of the tenant (D-96).
      - `::test_r1_evidence_digests`: intact bytes → `MATCH`; a `known_at`-only byte change → `INPUT_EVIDENCE_HASH_MISMATCH` although the logical hash matches; a hashed-field byte change → both mismatches; missing evidence → `INPUT_EVIDENCE_MISSING`; disagreeing T-CON-08 book hashes → `EXPECTED_HASH_DISAGREEMENT` (D-96).
      - `::test_r5_scope_set_authority`: an actor scoped to entity A cannot approve an attempt whose `required_entity_ids` is {A, B}; a delegation whose delegator lacks B fails; a revoked grant fails at decision time; SYSTEM and API clients are forbidden; stale content voids the request (D-96).
      - `::test_r6_attribution_preserves_engine_reasons`: the first computation after enablement posts into the first open period with S14 `LATE_EVENT` / `VOID` reasons intact and writes one T-SL-11 row per posting; an unsampled group is attributed; a second computation posts nothing (D-96).
      - `::test_r7_frozen_dataset_rerun`: re-running `upgrade_validation` after approval, retry and true-up reproduces the same dataset-derived totals and output hash while `engine_trueup_register` gains rows; a partial-scope reader sees filtered data and no output download (D-96).
      - `::test_a_runtime_unavailable_not_executed`: a source of another engine version stores the exact `SOURCE_RUNTIME_UNAVAILABLE` row with `engine_executed = false`, NULL executed identity and NULL actual hash, and a spy proves `compute` was not called; a missing transform → `NO_INPUT_TRANSFORM`; a same-version re-stamp → `MATCH`; a source already at the candidate version is validated (D-96 (A)).
      - `::test_a_executed_identity_check_refuses_malformed_rows`: the four named CHECKs refuse `engine_executed = true` with a NULL release id or version, `engine_executed = false` with a non-null release id, and an executed integrity replay without a source; the accepted unavailable-runtime row and a started-but-failed row with NULL actual output are stored (D-96 (A), v4).
      - `::test_b_orchestration_terminal_states_and_tokens`: `ERROR` → retry → `MATCH` with both rows retained and the pointer on the `MATCH`; exhausted retries → `FAILED_EXHAUSTED` → attempt `INCOMPLETE`; a cancelled child → `CANCELLED` → `INCOMPLETE`; duplicate redelivery of the same task → the same row, no second insert; a replaced stale task is fenced out; a candidate stored-hash corruption → `MISMATCH`, `INCOMPLETE`, no approval subject (D-96 (B)).
      - `::test_b_execution_lock_two_jobs_same_group`: two distinct child jobs for the same group, the second deferred while the first is RUNNING → the second waits on the execution lock (Procrastinate `lock` plus `pg_advisory_xact_lock` in the writing transaction), then finds the accepted result and writes nothing new (D-96 (B), v4).
      - `::test_c_bootstrap_requires_baseline`: a genuinely empty tenant auto-passes as `BOOTSTRAP` with the recorded zero counts; a tenant with old heads and postings but no validation rows does not; an unavailable preparation → `PREPARATION_MISSING` `INCOMPLETE`; rollback to the preparation artifact and redeploy resumes the same baseline without overwriting evidence (D-96 (C)).
      - `::test_c_baseline_names_preparation_release`: the preparation artifact is stamped as its own `engine_release` row (same `engine_version`, new `build_sha`) and the `BASELINE` row names that row as `engine_release_id` with `source_engine_release_id` = the historical build, so the artifact's own recomputations pass the per-process-release gate; `BASELINE`, `BOOTSTRAP` and `PATCH` rows seed no T-PLT-46 portions (D-96 (C/E), v4).
      - `::test_e_upgrade_processing_states_per_portion`: a group spanning entities A and B: A posts → only A's portion `POSTED` and attributed, B `DEFERRED_NO_OPEN_PERIOD` with its unresolved origin period keys, group not terminal, head moved; B's period opens → SCH-06 dirty → a later computation posts B → B `POSTED` with a T-SL-11 row although not the first computation → group terminal; a trace-only approved difference → every portion `SETTLED_NO_POSTING`, no ledger row; an ordinary zero delta with every origin assignable → `SETTLED_NO_POSTING`; a duplicate computation appends nothing; `BASELINE`/`BOOTSTRAP`/`PATCH` rows seed no portions (D-96 (E), v4).
      - `::test_e_partial_origin_deferral_jan_mar_jun`: same portion, January closed / March reopened / June closed with no later postable period → the January carry posts into March with a T-SL-11 row and the portion appends `DEFERRED_NO_OPEN_PERIOD` with `unresolved_origin_period_keys = {June}` and the March posting id, not `POSTED`; July opens → June posts into July with a T-SL-11 row → `POSTED` (D-96 (E), v5; Codex v4 review).
      - `::test_f_cause_labels_known_at_and_period_state`: a `known_at`-only change → the attribution views differ → `UPGRADE_AND_OTHER_INPUTS` while the CV-25 views are equal; a period-state-only change → `UPGRADE_AND_OTHER_INPUTS`; byte-equal attribution views → `UPGRADE_ONLY`; a previous head without evidence or without a transform chain → `UPGRADE_CONTEXT`; amounts identical under every label (D-96 (F), v4).
      - `backend/tests/architecture/test_registries_complete.py::test_dg_arc_08_job_registries` (re-run): `REPLAY_VERIFY` is no longer pending.

- [ ] **SOP-7 Audit coverage of the required actions.**
  - **Prerequisites:** SOP-6
  - **Scope:**
    - Paths: `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_audit_coverage.py`; `backend/tests/support/audit_catalogue.py` (the REQ-PLT-019 categories mapped to the audit actions the product writes); fixes in any command handler that lacks its audit event
    - Schema: none
    - API: none
    - Engine: none
    - Screens: none
  - **Acceptance:**
    - Tests:
      - `backend/tests/domain/platform/test_audit_coverage.py::test_ctl_038_every_command_route_writes_audit`: for every POST, PUT, PATCH and DELETE operation of the committed `docs/api/openapi.json` other than (a) `POST /session/login`, `POST /session/mfa`, the password-reset routes, `POST /oauth/token` and `POST /operator/tenants`, (b) stateless evaluations that return a result and persist nothing (the evaluate and explain-verify operations; a preview that defers a job is not one, see below) and (c) the 04 §1.7 AUD-OPS personal read markers, preferences and saved views (`me_notifications_read`, `me_notifications_read_all`, `me_notification_preferences_put`, `me_preferences_update`; `saved_views_create`, `saved_views_update`, `saved_views_delete`, rev 1.25), a successful call in a fresh tenant writes at least one `audit_event` in the same transaction, with `action` matching `^[a-z_]+\.[a-z_]+$`, `request_id` equal to the response `X-Request-Id`, and `actor_kind` of the caller; the routes of (a) write `security_event` rows instead (login, failed login, MFA, password reset) or the provisioning events of DG-KRN-TEN-01. The exemptions of (b) and (c) are defined by class here and enumerated as the `query` class of `backend/tests/support/audit_catalogue.py`, each named in the test's failure message and in the lane record; the walk additionally asserts that a `query` operation persists nothing except the 04 §1.7 personal rows (zero `audit_event` rows and zero other writes), so the label can never hide a mutation. The persistence boundary the walk measures is explicit: the `idempotency_record` row that `run_command` writes for every command (04 §1.7 AUD-OPS; `idempotency/store.py`) is infrastructure allowed on every path, `query` operations included, and nothing else is — a queued `job` row is not routine infrastructure. A command that starts a job is a job-starting command in the audited class, not a stateless evaluation: `POST /contracts/{id}/events/preview` and `POST /estimate-versions/{id}/preview` defer a `CONTRACT_COMPUTE` dry run (`uow.defer` inserts the `job` row in the command's transaction and dispatches after commit) and return 202, so they leave class (b); 04 T-PLT-27 AUD-OPS audits the commands that start and finish jobs, and their audit evidence is the job-start `audit_event` written in the same transaction as the `job` row (the jobs registry writes it on every deferral of a unit of work: `job.start`, as the caller and under the command's request id — 04 T-PLT-27 rev 1.106, supervisor rulings R-50 (a) and R-62 (a), header rev 1.37; until then no deferral left an audit event and the three previews, `POST /jobs/{id}/cancel` and `POST /audit-events/verify` failed this walk). The exemption is not widened for queued work, and the catalogue's exact-set unit test is a declaration, not proof: only the walk proves that a `query` operation writes nothing (D-98 candidate 49, supervisor ruling of 2026-09-20). `POST /session/invitations/lookup` is not exempt: a lookup with an unknown, expired or malformed token is a probing signal and writes a `security_event` (an invitation-token failure kind of the E-79 vocabulary); a successful lookup writes none (D-98 (20), supervisor ruling of 2026-09-19). Class (d), idempotent confirms (SOP7-PUBLISH-CONFIRM-1; rev 1.16): `POST /account-mappings/{id}/publish`, `POST /pob-template-versions/{id}/publish`, `POST /rule-set-versions/{id}/publish`, `POST /policies/{id}/publish` and `POST /import-mapping-profiles/{id}/publish` stay REQUIRED but their state change is performed and audited by the approval that precedes them (04 §16.5: the final approval publishes in the approval transaction; `lifecycle.approve` and `mapping.approve` call `publish`, which returns a PUBLISHED version as it is), so for them "at least one `audit_event` in the same transaction, with `request_id` equal to the response `X-Request-Id`, and `actor_kind` of the caller" is read on the APPROVAL transaction: the `<table>.published` row under the FINAL approving request's `X-Request-Id` and `actor_kind`, never on the confirm's own request, which writes no transition audit and only the `idempotency_record` infrastructure row. The walk credits a class-(d) operation only when it correlates, in one tenant, the table, the version-row UUID, the business approval id, the final approving request id, the SUCCESS `APPROVED → PUBLISHED` event and its actor, then issues a DISTINCT fresh confirmation (a fresh `Idempotency-Key`, not an idempotent replay) that returns the same PUBLISHED version, and compares the bounded subject state (status, `row_version`, `updated_at`, content hash) before and after the confirmation as unchanged; zero audit rows alone proves neither the absence of an update nor the handler's execution. The class is enumerated as the `confirm` class of `backend/tests/support/audit_catalogue.py` with its publication literal noted as f-string-built (`lifecycle.transition`: `f"{kind.object_type}.{status}"`) and verified at runtime, not statically. The `APPROVED`-left-unpublished `publish` branch (a version whose approval did not publish it) is a separate path the class does not cover. For the signature-verified webhook receiver (`webhooks_receive`, ADP-01; rev 1.19) the recorded actor is the tenant's system principal, `actor_kind` SYSTEM: the unauthenticated receiver's caller is the adapter and the product records it under `system_principal` by design, so the walk's caller-kind check admits SYSTEM for that one operation and names it in its failure message. Saved views (rev 1.25; supervisor ruling R-14 of 2026-09-29): class (c) is defined by the 04 §1.7 AUD-OPS class, and 04 T-PLT-37 classes `saved_view` — a user's saved grid filters, columns and favourites — AUD-OPS, so `POST /saved-views`, `PATCH /saved-views/{id}` and `DELETE /saved-views/{id}` belong to it; its first enumeration omitted them. The walk's persistence assertion holds for them as for every class (c) operation: zero `audit_event` rows, and nothing written but the caller's own `saved_view` row — the operation's 04 §1.7 personal row — and the `idempotency_record` infrastructure row. Class (e), a command the release refuses by name, which stores nothing (rev 1.44; item POLICY-OVERRIDE-WITHDRAW-1, register index 308; supervisor ruling R-126 (b) (4) and (c) and the supervisor's rulings of 2026-10-03 on the lane's line; 04 T-CON-23 "Not offered in release 1.0" rev 1.322; PRD ERR-102): `POST /policy-overrides` stays a command operation of the OpenAPI document and refuses every creation, so no successful call of it exists to evidence. It stays REQUIRED and is walked for its refusal: the call of a person who holds the permission, on a contract she reads, must answer 422 `policy-level-not-allowed` with rule id `POLICY_OVERRIDE_NOT_OFFERED`, write zero `audit_event` rows under its request id and change the row count of no tenant table, the `idempotency_record` infrastructure row apart, as for class (b); any other answer — a success above all — an audit row or a changed table fails the walk by name. The class is enumerated as the `refused` class of `backend/tests/support/audit_catalogue.py` (`RELEASE_REFUSED`), each operation with the rule id it must answer; the catalogue's unit test pins the class to exactly this one operation, and the walk, not the pin, proves that nothing is stored. The action `policy_override.create` leaves the catalogue with the code that wrote it: the REQ-PLT-019 category of configuration, policy, mapping and SSP changes keeps its other five actions. `POST /policy-overrides/{id}/submit` stays in the audited class; the walk's world writes the DRAFT row it submits (`support.rows.insert_policy_override`), since the product creates none.
      - `::test_req_plt_019_categories_covered`: the audit actions observed while running one end-to-end fixture scenario include, per REQ-PLT-019 category: login and logout (`security_event` `LOGIN_SUCCEEDED`, `LOGIN_FAILED`, `LOGOUT`), MFA changes (`MFA_ENROLLED`, `MFA_RESET`), role, permission and SoD changes (`role.create`, `role_assignment.create`, `sod_exception.create`), configuration, policy, mapping and SSP changes (`registry_version.publish`, `ssp_book_version.approve`, `account_mapping_version.publish`), imports (`import_upload.upload`, `import_upload.validate`, `import_upload.approve`, `import_upload.commit`), events submitted and voided (`contract_event.append`, `contract_event.void`), approvals and rejections (`approval_request.approve`, `approval_request.reject`), calculation and close runs (`contract_computation.persist`, `close_run.start`), period status changes (`period_state.closed`), journal lifecycle (`journal_run.approve`, `journal_batch.export`), report runs and exports (`report_run.create`), API client lifecycle (`api_client.create`, `api_client.revoke`), support grants (`support_grant.approve`) and AI proposal lifecycle (`ai_proposal.accept`). Each name is taken from the handler that implements it; a missing category fails the test with the category name.
      - `::test_denied_commands_audited`: for five command routes called by a principal lacking the permission, one `DENIED` audit event exists per call.
    - Answer keys: none
    - Golden: none
